      g.beginPath(); g.arc(p.x,p.y,on?6.5:4.5,0,Math.PI*2); g.fillStyle = ED.lock ? (on ? '#12B886' : '#3fd6a6') : (on ? '#2F5CE8' : '#5b7cf0'); g.fill();
    }
  }
}
function edResize(){
  const st = document.getElementById('edStage'), r = st.getBoundingClientRect(); if(r.width < 10 || r.height < 10) return;
  ED.dpr = window.devicePixelRatio || 1; ED.cv.width = Math.round(r.width*ED.dpr); ED.cv.height = Math.round(r.height*ED.dpr); ED.cw = r.width; ED.ch = r.height;
  if(ED.needFit){ ED.needFit = false; edFit(); return; }
  edDraw();
}
function edFit(){
  const W = edWorld(); let x0 = 1e9, y0 = 1e9, x1 = -1e9, y1 = -1e9;
  ED.order.forEach(k=>{ const a = edArt(k); if(!a) return; [[0,0],[a.w,0],[a.w,a.h],[0,a.h]].forEach(([lx,ly])=>{ const p = edL2W(W,k,lx,ly); x0 = Math.min(x0,p.x); x1 = Math.max(x1,p.x); y0 = Math.min(y0,p.y); y1 = Math.max(y1,p.y); }); });
  const bw = Math.max(1,x1-x0), bh = Math.max(1,y1-y0), z = Math.min((ED.cw-36)/bw, (ED.ch-36)/bh);
  ED.cam.z = Math.max(0.05, z); ED.cam.x = ED.cw/2 - (x0+x1)/2*ED.cam.z; ED.cam.y = ED.ch/2 - (y0+y1)/2*ED.cam.z;
  edSyncZoom(); edDraw();
}
function edSyncZoom(){ const e = document.getElementById('edZoomVal'); if(e) e.textContent = Math.round(ED.cam.z*100)+'%'; }
function edZoomAt(f, cx, cy){ const z = Math.max(0.08, Math.min(8, ED.cam.z*f)), k = z/ED.cam.z; ED.cam.x = cx-(cx-ED.cam.x)*k; ED.cam.y = cy-(cy-ED.cam.y)*k; ED.cam.z = z; edSyncZoom(); edDraw(); }
function edZoomBy(f){ edZoomAt(f, ED.cw/2, ED.ch/2); }

/* ---------- undo / redo ---------- */
const edSnap = ()=>JSON.stringify({ st:ED.st, order:ED.order, assign:ED.assign, facing:ED.facing });
function edRestore(j){ const o = JSON.parse(j); ED.st = o.st; ED.order = o.order; ED.assign = o.assign; ED.facing = o.facing === -1 ? -1 : 1; }
function edPush(){ ED.undo.push(edSnap()); if(ED.undo.length > 80) ED.undo.shift(); ED.redo.length = 0; edSyncHistory(); }
function edUndo(){ if(!ED.undo.length) return; ED.redo.push(edSnap()); edRestore(ED.undo.pop()); edAfterChange(true); }
function edRedo(){ if(!ED.redo.length) return; ED.undo.push(edSnap()); edRestore(ED.redo.pop()); edAfterChange(true); }
function edSyncHistory(){ document.getElementById('edUndoBtn').disabled = !ED.undo.length; document.getElementById('edRedoBtn').disabled = !ED.redo.length; }
function edAfterChange(full){ edSyncHistory(); if(full) edSyncUI(); else edSyncVals(); edDraw(); }

/* ---------- selection + toolbar ---------- */
function edThumb(a){
  if(!a.thumb){ const k = 60/Math.max(a.w,a.h), c = document.createElement('canvas'); c.width = Math.max(1,Math.round(a.w*k)); c.height = Math.max(1,Math.round(a.h*k)); c.getContext('2d').drawImage(a.canvas,0,0,c.width,c.height); a.thumb = c.toDataURL('image/png'); }
  return a.thumb;
}
// v328 PART LOCK: a locked part can't be selected (chip or canvas tap), moved, resized, rotated or bulk-edited until it is
// unlocked again from its long-press "Part options" panel. State lives in ED.lockedParts and is saved with the model.
function edIsLocked(k){ return !!(k && ED.lockedParts && ED.lockedParts[k]); }
function edSetPartLocked(k, on){
  if(!k || !ED.st || !ED.st[k]) return false;
  if(!ED.lockedParts) ED.lockedParts = {};
  if(on){ ED.lockedParts[k] = true; if(ED.sel === k) ED.sel = null; }
  else delete ED.lockedParts[k];
  edSyncUI(); edDraw();
  showToast(sheetLabel(k)+(on ? ' locked' : ' unlocked'), { duration:1300 });
  return true;
}
function edSelect(k){
  if(edIsLocked(k)){ showToast(sheetLabel(k)+' is locked. Long press it to unlock.', { duration:1500 }); return; }
  ED.sel = k; edSyncUI(); edDraw();
}
// Facial reactions, mouths and each side's hand gestures are interchangeable variants. Paired
// arms/legs share size and rotation edits without destroying their different anatomical positions.
function edSimilarKeys(k){
  // v333: every part family now has a rule, for humanoid, animal and bird models alike.
  // Head variants (incl. the open-eye head), mouths (incl. mouth_hold), hands (incl. holding hands).
  if(ED_SIM.head.test(k)) return SHEET_KEYS.filter(x=>ED_SIM.head.test(x));
  if(ED_SIM.mouth.test(k)) return ['mouth_closed','mouth_open','mouth_hold'];
  // v318: open eyes (eye_L/eye_R) and closed eyes (eye_closed_L/eye_closed_R) are left/right pairs.
  const em=k.match(/^(eye|eye_closed)_([LR])$/);
  if(em) return [em[1]+'_L',em[1]+'_R'];
  // v333: eyebrows are a left/right pair (humanoid + animal face kits).
  if(/^brow_[LR]$/.test(k)) return ['brow_L','brow_R'];
  // Hand transforms are a paired character-level edit, not side-specific: include both base hands
  // and every matching gesture on both sides. Per-part defaults still preserve each gesture's own
  // wrist pivot and the left/right model's original mirror direction.
  if(ED_SIM.hand.test(k)) return SHEET_KEYS.filter(x=>ED_SIM.hand.test(x));
  const lm=k.match(/^(arm|leg)([LR])_(upper|lower)$/);
  if(lm){ const other=lm[2]==='L'?'R':'L'; return [k,lm[1]+other+'_'+lm[3]]; }
  // v333: shoes are a left/right pair.
  if(/^shoe[LR]$/.test(k)) return ['shoeL','shoeR'];
  // v333: bird flight wings are a left/right pair; bird eye and beak states are interchangeable variants.
  if(/^wing[LR]_fly$/.test(k)) return ['wingL_fly','wingR_fly'];
  if(/^bird_eye(?:_closed)?$/.test(k)) return ['bird_eye','bird_eye_closed'];
  if(/^beak_(?:closed|open)$/.test(k)) return ['beak_closed','beak_open'];
  return [k];
}
// v333: shared matchers so "which parts are similar" and "how to transfer the edit" never disagree.
const ED_SIM={
  // v371: head variants include the emotion heads (sad / angry / shocked / laughing) - they were missing, so "Apply similar" found no matches.
  head:/^head(?:_(?:blink|open|sad|angry|shock|laugh))?$/,
  mouth:/^mouth_(?:closed|open|hold)$/,
  hand:/^hand[LR](?:_(?:fist|point|thumb|palm|hold))?$/,
  birdVariant:/^(?:bird_eye(?:_closed)?|beak_(?:closed|open))$/
};
function edApplyToSimilarParts(){
  const k=ED.sel;
  if(!k || !ED.st[k]){ showToast('Select a part first',{duration:1300}); return; }
  let init;
  try{ init = typeof ED.init === 'string' ? JSON.parse(ED.init).st : ED.init.st; }
  catch(e){ showToast('Could not read the starting layout',{duration:1500}); return; }
  const headGroup=ED_SIM.head.test(k);
  const keys=edSimilarKeys(k);
  const group=keys.filter(x=>x!==k && !edIsLocked(x) && ED.st[x] && init[x] && ED.arts[ED.assign[x]]);
  if(!group.length){ showToast(keys.length<=1 ? 'This part has no matching pair to copy to' : 'No other matching parts are present in this sheet',{duration:1500}); return; }
  const src=ED.st[k], src0=init[k], srcArt=edArt(k);
  if(!src0 || !srcArt){ showToast('Could not read the selected part',{duration:1500}); return; }
  const ratio=(v,b)=>Math.abs(b)>1e-6?v/b:1;
  const variants=headGroup || ED_SIM.mouth.test(k) || ED_SIM.hand.test(k) || ED_SIM.birdVariant.test(k);
  let applied=0;
  edPush();
  group.forEach(q=>{
    const dst=ED.st[q], d0=init[q], art=edArt(q);
    if(!dst || !d0 || !art) return;
    const prevDst={...dst};
    // Transfer edit DELTAS from the selected piece, relative to its opening state. This preserves
    // the destination's own detected proportions while copying every intentional transform edit.
    dst.s=Math.max(.3,Math.min(3,d0.s*ratio(src.s,src0.s)));
    dst.wx=Math.max(.2,Math.min(4,d0.wx*ratio(src.wx,src0.wx)));
    dst.hy=Math.max(.2,Math.min(4,d0.hy*ratio(src.hy,src0.hy)));
    dst.rot=d0.rot+(src.rot-src0.rot);
    dst.fx=src.fx===src0.fx ? d0.fx : -d0.fx;
    dst.fy=src.fy===src0.fy ? d0.fy : -d0.fy;
    if(variants){
      const srcParent=SHEET_PARENT[k], dstParent=SHEET_PARENT[q];
      const pa=srcParent&&edArt(srcParent), pb=dstParent&&edArt(dstParent);
      const sx=pa&&pb ? pb.w/Math.max(1,pa.w) : 1;
      const sy=pa&&pb ? pb.h/Math.max(1,pa.h) : 1;
      const sp0=srcParent&&init[srcParent], dp0=dstParent&&init[dstParent];
      const sign=v=>v ? Math.sign(v.s*v.wx*v.fx)||1 : 1;
      const flipLocalX=ED.view!=='side' && sign(sp0)!==sign(dp0) ? -1 : 1;
      dst.ax=d0.ax+(src.ax-src0.ax)*sx*flipLocalX;
      dst.ay=d0.ay+(src.ay-src0.ay)*sy;
    } else {
      const mirror=ED.view!=='side';
      dst.ax=d0.ax+(mirror?-(src.ax-src0.ax):(src.ax-src0.ax));
      dst.ay=d0.ay+(src.ay-src0.ay);
    }
    // Keep each part's own pivot but copy only the selected part's pivot CHANGE (relative to its opening state).
    // FIX: this used to be measured from the art's default pivot (dpx/dpy) while ax/ay were measured from the
    // opening layout, so any model with an already-moved pivot made the matching part jump away / seem to vanish.
    dst.pvx=d0.pvx+(src.pvx-src0.pvx)*(art.w/Math.max(1,srcArt.w));
    dst.pvy=d0.pvy+(src.pvy-src0.pvy)*(art.h/Math.max(1,srcArt.h));
    // Safety net: never let a bad number hide a part. If anything went non-finite, put this part back as it was.
    if(!['s','wx','hy','rot','fx','fy','ax','ay','pvx','pvy'].every(f=>Number.isFinite(dst[f]))){ Object.assign(dst, prevDst); return; }
    applied++;
  });
  if(!applied){ ED.undo.pop(); showToast('No matching parts could be updated',{duration:1500}); return; }
  edAfterChange(true);
  showToast(headGroup ? 'Applied head edits to '+applied+' head variants' : 'Applied edits to '+applied+' matching parts',{duration:1800});
}
function edSyncUI(){
  const box = document.getElementById('edLayers');
  box.innerHTML = ED.order.slice().reverse().map(k=>`<button class="ed-chip ${k===ED.sel?'is-on':''} ${edIsLocked(k)?'is-locked':''}" data-k="${k}" data-locked="${edIsLocked(k)?'1':'0'}" onclick="edSelect('${k}')"><span class="th"><img src="${edThumb(edArt(k))}" alt="">${edIsLocked(k)?'<i class="ed-lock-badge" aria-label="Locked"><svg viewBox="0 0 24 24" width="10" height="10" fill="none" stroke="currentColor" stroke-width="2.6" stroke-linecap="round" stroke-linejoin="round"><rect x="5" y="11" width="14" height="9.5" rx="2.2"/><path d="M8.2 11V8a3.8 3.8 0 0 1 7.6 0v3"/></svg></i>':''}</span><span class="n">${sheetLabel(k)}</span></button>`).join('');
  const on = box.querySelector('.is-on'); if(on) on.scrollIntoView({ inline:'nearest', block:'nearest' });
  document.getElementById('edSubtitle').textContent = ED.sel ? sheetLabel(ED.sel) + ' selected' : 'Tap a part to edit it';
  document.getElementById('edSwapBtn').disabled = !ED.sel || !/[LR]_/.test(ED.sel);
  const fmb = document.getElementById('edFlipModelBtn'); if(fmb) fmb.style.display = ED.view === 'side' ? '' : 'none';
  document.querySelectorAll('#edModes button').forEach(b=>b.classList.toggle('is-on', b.dataset.m === ED.mode));
  const bb = document.getElementById('edBonesBtn'); bb.classList.toggle('is-on', ED.bones); bb.classList.toggle('is-off', !ED.bones); bb.innerHTML = edIcon(ED.bones ? 'eye' : 'eyeOff');
  const bg = document.getElementById('edLockBadge'); if(bg){ bg.classList.remove('show'); bg.textContent = ''; }
  const lb = document.getElementById('edLockBtn'); lb.classList.toggle('is-lock', ED.lock); lb.innerHTML = edIcon(ED.lock ? 'lock' : 'unlock'); lb.setAttribute('aria-pressed', ED.lock ? 'true' : 'false');
  edSyncHistory(); edSyncVals();
}
const ED_RANGE = { size:[30,300,'%'], width:[30,300,'%'], height:[30,300,'%'], rotate:[-180,180,'°'], position:[-150,150,'%'] };
// The part's auto-placed position when the sheet was first opened -- the 0% reference point for the
// Move sliders, and what "Reset part" already returns ax/ay to, so the two stay conceptually in sync.
function edInitSt(k){ try{ return JSON.parse(ED.init).st[k]; }catch(e){ return null; } }
// Position is expressed as a percentage of the part's OWN width/height (not raw pixels) so the same
// slider range feels right whether you're nudging a tiny mouth or the whole torso.
function edPosVal(axis){
  const st = ED.st[ED.sel], init = edInitSt(ED.sel), a = edArt(ED.sel); if(!st || !init || !a) return 0;
  const span = axis==='x' ? a.w : a.h; if(!span) return 0;
  const cur = axis==='x' ? st.ax : st.ay, base = axis==='x' ? init.ax : init.ay;
  return Math.round((cur-base)/span*100);
}
function edApplyPos(axis, v){
  const st = ED.st[ED.sel], init = edInitSt(ED.sel), a = edArt(ED.sel); if(!st || !init || !a) return;
  const span = axis==='x' ? a.w : a.h;
  if(axis==='x'){ st.ax = init.ax + v/100*span; document.getElementById('edVal').textContent = 'X ' + v + '%'; }
  else { st.ay = init.ay + v/100*span; document.getElementById('edValY').textContent = 'Y ' + v + '%'; }
  edDraw();
}
function edGetVal(){
  const st = ED.st[ED.sel]; if(!st) return 0;
  if(ED.mode === 'size') return Math.round(st.s*100);
  if(ED.mode === 'width') return Math.round(st.wx*100);
  if(ED.mode === 'height') return Math.round(st.hy*100);
  if(ED.mode === 'rotate') return Math.round(st.rot);
  return edPosVal('x');   // position mode: the main (first) slider is X
}
function edSyncVals(){
  const rowY = document.getElementById('edSliderRowY');
  if(ED.mode === 'position'){
    rowY.style.display = '';
    const r = ED_RANGE.position;
    const slX = document.getElementById('edSlider'), vX = edPosVal('x');
    slX.min = r[0]; slX.max = r[1]; slX.value = vX; document.getElementById('edVal').textContent = 'X ' + vX + '%';
    const slY = document.getElementById('edSliderY'), vY = edPosVal('y');
    slY.min = r[0]; slY.max = r[1]; slY.value = vY; document.getElementById('edValY').textContent = 'Y ' + vY + '%';
  } else {
    rowY.style.display = 'none';
    const r = ED_RANGE[ED.mode], sl = document.getElementById('edSlider'), v = edGetVal();
    sl.min = r[0]; sl.max = r[1]; sl.value = v; document.getElementById('edVal').textContent = v + r[2];
  }
}
function edSetMode(m){ ED.mode = m; edSyncUI(); }
let edSliderPushed = false;
function edSliderStart(){ if(!edSliderPushed){ edPush(); edSliderPushed = true; } }
function edSliderEnd(){ edSliderPushed = false; }
function edSliderInput(v){
  const st = ED.st[ED.sel]; if(!st) return; v = +v;
  if(ED.mode === 'position'){ edApplyPos('x', v); return; }
  if(ED.mode==='size') st.s = v/100; else if(ED.mode==='width') st.wx = v/100; else if(ED.mode==='height') st.hy = v/100; else st.rot = v;
  document.getElementById('edVal').textContent = v + ED_RANGE[ED.mode][2]; edDraw();
}
function edSliderInputY(v){ if(ED.mode !== 'position' || !ED.sel) return; edApplyPos('y', +v); }
function edNudge(d){ if(!ED.sel) return; edPush(); const r = ED_RANGE[ED.mode]; edSliderInput(Math.max(r[0], Math.min(r[1], edGetVal()+d))); edSyncVals(); }
function edNudgeY(d){ if(!ED.sel || ED.mode !== 'position') return; edPush(); const r = ED_RANGE.position; edSliderInputY(Math.max(r[0], Math.min(r[1], edPosVal('y')+d))); edSyncVals(); }
function edFlip(axis){ if(!ED.sel) return; edPush(); const k = ED.sel, st = ED.st[k]; edKeepJoints(k, ()=>{ if(axis==='x') st.fx *= -1; else st.fy *= -1; }); edDraw(); }
// NEAR/FAR limb pairs in a side-view sheet: two pieces with the same silhouette (the far one a touch darker,
// see SHEET_PROMPT_SIDE's "NO MIRRORING IN SIDE VIEW" rule). When the model turns around, the near and far
// limbs trade places, so the far leg becomes the near one and vice versa.
const SHEET_FLIP_PAIRS = [['armL_upper','armR_upper'], ['armL_lower','armR_lower'], ['handL','handR'], ['legL_upper','legR_upper'], ['legL_lower','legR_lower'], ['shoeL','shoeR'], ['wingL','wingR'],
  ['handL_fist','handR_fist'], ['handL_point','handR_point'], ['handL_thumb','handR_thumb'], ['handL_palm','handR_palm']];
// Turns the WHOLE side-view model around so it faces the other way. Every piece turns, limbs and hands included:
// its art is reflected (fx), its joint is mirrored across the body's centre line and its angle is negated, so a foot
// or hand that pointed forward now points the new way. The near and far limbs first trade places together with their
// own state (art, size, stretch, rotation and joint edits), so nothing the person tuned is lost. Parents go first so
// children are placed against their new parent transforms. Side view only.
// ED.facing flips as well: the animation presets read it, so arms and legs swing toward the way the model now faces.
function edFlipModel(){
  if(ED.view !== 'side') return;
  edPush();
  SHEET_FLIP_PAIRS.forEach(([l,r])=>{
    if(!ED.arts[ED.assign[l]] || !ED.arts[ED.assign[r]]) return;   // basic sheets may not have the fist pair
    const t = ED.assign[l]; ED.assign[l] = ED.assign[r]; ED.assign[r] = t;
    const s = ED.st[l]; ED.st[l] = ED.st[r]; ED.st[r] = s;
  });
  const W0 = edWorld(true), cx = W0.torso.x, done = {};
  const go = k => {
    if(done[k] || !ED.st[k] || !W0[k]) return; done[k] = true;
    const p = SHEET_PARENT[k]; if(p) go(p);
    const st = ED.st[k], old = W0[k];
    st.fx *= -1;                                   // reflect this piece's art (limbs and hands included)
    if(p){
      const P = edWorld(true)[p];
      st.rot = (-old.rot - P.rot)*180/Math.PI;
      edSetPos(k, 2*cx - old.x, old.y);
    } else st.rot = -old.rot*180/Math.PI;
  };
  SHEET_KEYS.forEach(go);
  ED.facing = -(ED.facing || 1);
  edSyncVals(); edDraw();
  const flipBtn=document.getElementById('edFlipModelBtn');
  if(flipBtn){ flipBtn.setAttribute('aria-pressed', ED.facing < 0 ? 'true' : 'false'); flipBtn.title='Turn the entire side-profile rig 180 degrees. Near/far limbs swap, and every piece stays editable'; }
  showToast(ED.facing < 0 ? 'Model turned to face left' : 'Model turned to face right', { duration:1500 });
}
function edSwap(){
  const k = ED.sel; if(!k || !/[LR]_/.test(k)) return;
  const o = k.replace(/([LR])_/, (m,s)=>(s==='L'?'R':'L')+'_');
  if(edIsLocked(o)){ showToast(sheetLabel(o)+' is locked. Unlock it first.', { duration:1500 }); return; }
  edPush(); const t = ED.assign[k]; ED.assign[k] = ED.assign[o]; ED.assign[o] = t;
  [k,o].forEach(x=>{ const a = edArt(x); ED.st[x].pvx = a.dpx; ED.st[x].pvy = a.dpy; });
  edSyncUI(); edDraw(); showToast('Swapped '+sheetLabel(k).toLowerCase()+' and '+sheetLabel(o).toLowerCase(), { duration:1500 });
}
function edResetPart(){
  if(!ED.sel) return; const init = JSON.parse(ED.init), k = ED.sel; edPush();
  const a = edArt(k); const cur = ED.st[k], i0 = init.st[k];
  if(cur && i0 && (cur.fx !== i0.fx || cur.fy !== i0.fy)) edKeepJoints(k, ()=>{ cur.fx = i0.fx; cur.fy = i0.fy; });
  ED.st[k] = { ...init.st[k], pvx:a.dpx, pvy:a.dpy }; edAfterChange(true);
}
// v372 POSITION SHEET: writes the CURRENT position of every part of the model being edited (size, width, length,
// rotation, flips, joint attachment and pivot) plus the opening layout and draw order into a downloadable .json sheet.
// Hand this sheet back to lock these positions as the model's default layout.
function edPosSheetNum(v){ return Number.isFinite(v) ? Math.round(v*100000)/100000 : v; }
function edBuildPositionSheet(){
  let init={}; try{ init = typeof ED.init === 'string' ? JSON.parse(ED.init).st : (ED.init && ED.init.st) || {}; }catch(e){}
  const clean=o=>{ const r={}; Object.keys(o||{}).forEach(f=>{ const v=o[f]; if(typeof v==='number') r[f]=edPosSheetNum(v); else if(typeof v==='boolean'||typeof v==='string') r[f]=v; }); return r; };
  const parts={};
  Object.keys(ED.st||{}).forEach(k=>{
    const a=ED.arts && ED.arts[(ED.assign&&ED.assign[k])||k];
    parts[k]={ label:(typeof sheetLabel==='function'?sheetLabel(k):k), art:a?{ w:a.w, h:a.h, dpx:edPosSheetNum(a.dpx), dpy:edPosSheetNum(a.dpy) }:null, position:clean(ED.st[k]), startLayout:clean(init[k]), locked:!!(ED.lockedParts&&ED.lockedParts[k]) };
  });
  return { sheetType:'ethvelle-position-sheet', version:1, createdAt:new Date().toISOString(), modelName:ED.name||'Model', modelType:ED.modelType||'humanoid', view:ED.view, facing:ED.facing, dress:!!ED.dress, beast:!!ED.beast, partCount:Object.keys(parts).length, order:(ED.order||[]).slice(), parts };
}
function edDownloadPositionSheet(){
  if(!ED.open || !ED.st || !Object.keys(ED.st).length){ showToast('Open a model first',{duration:1400}); return; }
  try{
    const sheet=edBuildPositionSheet();
    const safe=String(sheet.modelName||'model').replace(/[^\w\-]+/g,'_').replace(/^_+|_+$/g,'').slice(0,40)||'model';
    const name='Position_Sheet_'+safe+'_'+sheet.modelType+'_'+sheet.view+'.json';
    const blob=new Blob([JSON.stringify(sheet,null,2)],{type:'application/json'});
    const url=URL.createObjectURL(blob), a=document.createElement('a');
    a.href=url; a.download=name; a.style.display='none'; document.body.appendChild(a); a.click();
    setTimeout(()=>{ a.remove(); URL.revokeObjectURL(url); },4000);
    showToast('Position sheet created ('+sheet.partCount+' parts)',{duration:2000});
  }catch(e){ showToast('Could not create the position sheet',{duration:1800}); }
}
function edResetAll(){ edPush(); const keepLocked = {}; Object.keys(ED.lockedParts||{}).forEach(k=>{ if(ED.lockedParts[k] && ED.st[k]) keepLocked[k] = JSON.parse(JSON.stringify(ED.st[k])); }); edRestore(ED.init); Object.keys(keepLocked).forEach(k=>{ if(ED.st[k]) ED.st[k] = keepLocked[k]; }); edAfterChange(true); edFit(); showToast('Reset to the starting layout', { duration:1300 }); }
// Only joint attachment coordinates and bone rotations are restored. Artwork scale, width, length,
// flips and the rest of the part edits remain untouched. The "default" is the pose/layout this
// specific model had when this editing session opened.
// v379: parts whose edited position/rotation must survive Done on a plain humanoid (head + both legs: thigh, shin, shoe).
function edKeepOnDone(k){ return k==='head' || /^(leg[LR]_(upper|lower)|shoe[LR])$/.test(String(k||'')); }
function edSkeletonAtDefault(keepHead){
  if(!ED.init || !ED.st) return false;
  let base; try{ base=JSON.parse(ED.init).st||{}; }catch(e){ return false; }
  for(const k of Object.keys(ED.st)){
    if(k==='tail' && ED.modelType==='humanoid') continue;   // v344: the beast-man tail keeps the rest pose chosen in the editor
    if(keepHead && edKeepOnDone(k)) continue;   // v378/v379: head and leg positions edited by the user are saved, never reset on Done
    // Mouth crops are independent face overlays nested inside the head, not skeleton joints.
    // Their ax/ay/rot are part of the model's saved facial layout and must not block the
    // skeleton-default check after a user deliberately repositions them.
    if(k==='mouth_closed'||k==='mouth_open'||k==='mouth_hold'||(typeof SHEET_FACE_NESTED!=='undefined'&&SHEET_FACE_NESTED.includes(k))||(typeof SHEET_FACE_KIT_NESTED!=='undefined'&&SHEET_FACE_KIT_NESTED.includes(k))||(typeof SHEET_HUMANOID_FACE_KIT_KEYS!=='undefined'&&SHEET_HUMANOID_FACE_KIT_KEYS.includes(k))||(typeof SHEET_BIRD_FACE_NESTED!=='undefined'&&SHEET_BIRD_FACE_NESTED.includes(k))||(typeof SHEET_ALT_OF!=='undefined'&&SHEET_ALT_OF[k])) continue;
    const cur=ED.st[k], ref=base[k]; if(!cur || !ref) continue;
    for(const prop of ['ax','ay','rot']){
      if(Number.isFinite(ref[prop]) && (!Number.isFinite(cur[prop]) || Math.abs(cur[prop]-ref[prop])>1e-4)) return false;
    }
  }
  return true;
}
function edResetSkeletonToDefault(silent, keepHead){
  if(!ED.open || !ED.init || !ED.st) return false;
  let base; try{ base=JSON.parse(ED.init).st||{}; }catch(e){ return false; }
  const keys=Object.keys(ED.st).filter(k=>{
    if(k==='tail' && ED.modelType==='humanoid') return false;   // v344: never reset the beast-man tail
    if(keepHead && edKeepOnDone(k)) return false;   // v378/v379: keep the edited head and leg positions (Done)
    // Reset only structural joint attachments. Keep mouth overlay transforms as edited by
    // the user; they are nested in the head and are not part of the skeleton pose.
    if(k==='mouth_closed'||k==='mouth_open'||k==='mouth_hold'||(typeof SHEET_FACE_NESTED!=='undefined'&&SHEET_FACE_NESTED.includes(k))||(typeof SHEET_FACE_KIT_NESTED!=='undefined'&&SHEET_FACE_KIT_NESTED.includes(k))||(typeof SHEET_HUMANOID_FACE_KIT_KEYS!=='undefined'&&SHEET_HUMANOID_FACE_KIT_KEYS.includes(k))||(typeof SHEET_BIRD_FACE_NESTED!=='undefined'&&SHEET_BIRD_FACE_NESTED.includes(k))||(typeof SHEET_ALT_OF!=='undefined'&&SHEET_ALT_OF[k])) return false;
    const cur=ED.st[k], ref=base[k];
    return !!(cur && ref && ['ax','ay','rot'].some(prop=>Number.isFinite(ref[prop]) && (!Number.isFinite(cur[prop]) || Math.abs(cur[prop]-ref[prop])>1e-4)));
  });
  if(keys.length){
    edPush();
    keys.forEach(k=>['ax','ay','rot'].forEach(prop=>{
      const cur=ED.st[k], ref=base[k];
      if(cur && ref && Number.isFinite(ref[prop])) cur[prop]=ref[prop];
    }));
    if(!ED.st[ED.sel]) ED.sel=ED.st.torso?'torso':Object.keys(ED.st)[0]||null;
    edAfterChange(true);
  }
  const ok=edSkeletonAtDefault(keepHead);
  if(!silent) showToast(ok ? (keys.length?'Skeleton reset to its default position':'Skeleton is already in its default position') : 'Could not reset this skeleton', {duration:1600});
  return ok;
}
function edToggleBones(){ ED.bones = !ED.bones; edSyncUI(); edDraw(); showToast(ED.bones ? 'Bones shown' : 'Bones hidden' + (ED.lock ? ' (still locked)' : ''), { duration:1100 }); }
// Bone <-> body-part lock. OFF: a joint dot moves on its own (the art stays where it is, so you can line the bones up with the art).
// ON: the body part is locked to its bone, so dragging a joint moves the part with it; an elbow / knee dot swings and stretches
// the whole arm / leg (upper part + lower part) so the dot stays under your finger.
function edToggleLock(){
  ED.lock = !ED.lock; if(ED.lock && !ED.bones) ED.bones = true;
  edSyncUI(); edDraw();
  // No floating green/black lock notification: the lock button state and aligned green joints
  // are the visual feedback, and locked mode now accepts only joint-point drags.
}

/* ---------- gestures ---------- */
function edSetupPointer(){
  const cv = ED.cv, ptrs = new Map(); let drag = null, moved = false;
  const pos = e=>{ const r = cv.getBoundingClientRect(); return { x:e.clientX-r.left, y:e.clientY-r.top }; };
  const toFig = p=>({ x:(p.x-ED.cam.x)/ED.cam.z, y:(p.y-ED.cam.y)/ED.cam.z });
  const begin = ()=>{ if(!moved){ moved = true; edPush(); } };
  cv.onpointerdown = e=>{
    cv.setPointerCapture(e.pointerId); const p = pos(e); ptrs.set(e.pointerId, p); moved = false;
    if(ptrs.size === 2){ const [a,b] = [...ptrs.values()]; drag = { type:'pinch', d:Math.hypot(a.x-b.x,a.y-b.y)||1, z:ED.cam.z, c:{x:(a.x+b.x)/2,y:(a.y+b.y)/2}, vx:ED.cam.x, vy:ED.cam.y }; return; }
    const W = edWorld(), hit = edPick(p, W), fp = toFig(p);
    if(hit.type === 'locked'){ drag = null; return; }
    if(hit.type === 'pan'){ drag = { type:'pan', p0:p, vx:ED.cam.x, vy:ED.cam.y, dist:0 }; return; }
    if(hit.key !== ED.sel){ ED.sel = hit.key; edSyncUI(); }
    const k = hit.key, w = W[k], st = ED.st[k];
    const isFaceOverlay=k==='mouth_closed' || k==='mouth_open' || k==='mouth_hold';
    if(hit.type === 'tailtip'){
      const tip = edTailTip(W);
      drag = { type:'tailtip', k:k, cx:w.x, cy:w.y, a0:Math.atan2(tip.y-w.y, tip.x-w.x), d0:Math.max(1, Math.hypot(tip.x-w.x, tip.y-w.y)), r0:st.rot, wx0:st.wx, hy0:st.hy };
    }
    else if(hit.type === 'move' && ED.lock && k !== 'torso' && !isFaceOverlay) drag = { type:'prot', k, cx:w.x, cy:w.y, a0:Math.atan2(fp.y-w.y, fp.x-w.x), r0:st.rot };
    else if(hit.type === 'move') drag = { type:'move', k, fp0:fp, x0:w.x, y0:w.y };
    else if(hit.type === 'pivot' && ED.lock){
      const off = { x:w.x-fp.x, y:w.y-fp.y };                                   // grab offset, so the dot doesn't jump under the finger
      if(/_lower$/.test(k) || /^hand[LR](?:_(?:fist|point|thumb|palm))?$/.test(k) || /^shoe[LR]$/.test(k)){
        const driver = SHEET_ALT_OF[k] || k;
        if(driver !== k && /^hand[LR](?:_(?:fist|point|thumb|palm))?$/.test(k)) drag = { type:'bone', k, driver, off };
        else drag = { type:'bone', k, driver, off };
        ED.sel = k; edSyncUI();
      } else drag = { type:'pmove', k, off };                                     // root/shoulder/hip/neck joint: move attached chain
    }
    else if(hit.type === 'pivot'){ const c = Math.cos(w.rot), s = Math.sin(w.rot); drag = { type:'pivot', k, ax:w.x-(st.pvx*w.sx*c-st.pvy*w.sy*s), ay:w.y-(st.pvx*w.sx*s+st.pvy*w.sy*c) }; }
    else if(hit.type === 'rot') drag = { type:'rot', k, x:w.x, y:w.y, v0:{ x:fp.x-w.x, y:fp.y-w.y }, s0:st.s, r0:st.rot };
    else if(hit.type === 'len'){ const dir = { x:-Math.sin(w.rot)*Math.sign(w.sy), y:Math.cos(w.rot)*Math.sign(w.sy) }; drag = { type:'len', k, x:w.x, y:w.y, dir, u0:(fp.x-w.x)*dir.x+(fp.y-w.y)*dir.y, h0:st.hy }; }
    else if(hit.type === 'wid'){ const dir = { x:Math.cos(w.rot)*Math.sign(w.sx), y:Math.sin(w.rot)*Math.sign(w.sx) }; drag = { type:'wid', k, x:w.x, y:w.y, dir, u0:(fp.x-w.x)*dir.x+(fp.y-w.y)*dir.y, w0:st.wx }; }
    edDraw();
  };
  cv.onpointermove = e=>{
    if(!ptrs.has(e.pointerId)) return; const p = pos(e); ptrs.set(e.pointerId, p); if(!drag) return;
    if(drag.type === 'pinch'){
      if(ptrs.size < 2) return; const [a,b] = [...ptrs.values()], d = Math.hypot(a.x-b.x,a.y-b.y), c = {x:(a.x+b.x)/2,y:(a.y+b.y)/2};
      const z = Math.max(0.08, Math.min(8, drag.z*d/drag.d)), k = z/drag.z; ED.cam.z = z; ED.cam.x = c.x-(drag.c.x-drag.vx)*k; ED.cam.y = c.y-(drag.c.y-drag.vy)*k; edSyncZoom(); edDraw(); return;
    }
    if(drag.type === 'pan'){ ED.cam.x = drag.vx+p.x-drag.p0.x; ED.cam.y = drag.vy+p.y-drag.p0.y; drag.dist = Math.max(drag.dist, Math.hypot(p.x-drag.p0.x, p.y-drag.p0.y)); edDraw(); return; }
    const fp = toFig(p), st = ED.st[drag.k];
    if(drag.type === 'move'){ begin(); edSetPos(drag.k, drag.x0+fp.x-drag.fp0.x, drag.y0+fp.y-drag.fp0.y); }
    else if(drag.type === 'prot'){          // locked: the part stays on its joint and swings around it (children follow)
      const vx = fp.x-drag.cx, vy = fp.y-drag.cy; if(Math.hypot(vx,vy) < 6/ED.cam.z) return;
      begin(); let r = drag.r0 + (Math.atan2(vy,vx)-drag.a0)*180/Math.PI; st.rot = ((r+180)%360+360)%360-180;
    }
    else if(drag.type === 'tailtip'){
      const vx = fp.x-drag.cx, vy = fp.y-drag.cy, d = Math.hypot(vx,vy); if(d < 6/ED.cam.z) return;
      begin();
      let r = drag.r0 + (Math.atan2(vy,vx)-drag.a0)*180/Math.PI; st.rot = ((r+180)%360+360)%360-180;
      if(ED.modelType==='humanoid'||isMythicModelType(ED.modelType)) st.hy = Math.max(0.3, Math.min(3, (drag.hy0||1)*d/drag.d0));   // upright tail segments stretch along their length (y)
      else st.wx = Math.max(0.3, Math.min(3, drag.wx0*d/drag.d0));
    }
    else if(drag.type === 'pmove'){ begin(); edSetPos(drag.k, fp.x+drag.off.x, fp.y+drag.off.y); }
    else if(drag.type === 'bone'){ begin(); edBoneDrive(drag.driver || drag.k, fp.x+drag.off.x, fp.y+drag.off.y); }
    else if(drag.type === 'pivot'){
      begin(); const w = edWorld()[drag.k], c = Math.cos(w.rot), s = Math.sin(w.rot), vx = fp.x-drag.ax, vy = fp.y-drag.ay;
      st.pvx = (vx*c+vy*s)/w.sx; st.pvy = (-vx*s+vy*c)/w.sy; edSetPos(drag.k, fp.x, fp.y);
    }
    else if(drag.type === 'rot'){
      begin(); const v = { x:fp.x-drag.x, y:fp.y-drag.y }, l0 = Math.hypot(drag.v0.x,drag.v0.y)||1, l1 = Math.hypot(v.x,v.y)||1;
      st.s = Math.max(0.3, Math.min(3, drag.s0*l1/l0));
      let r = drag.r0 + (Math.atan2(v.y,v.x)-Math.atan2(drag.v0.y,drag.v0.x))*180/Math.PI; r = ((r+180)%360+360)%360-180; st.rot = r;
    }
    else if(drag.type === 'len' || drag.type === 'wid'){
      begin(); const u = (fp.x-drag.x)*drag.dir.x+(fp.y-drag.y)*drag.dir.y;
      if(Math.abs(drag.u0) > 1){ const f = Math.max(0.2, Math.min(4, (drag.type==='len'?drag.h0:drag.w0)*u/drag.u0)); if(drag.type==='len') st.hy = f; else st.wx = f; }
    }
    edSyncVals(); edDraw();
  };
  cv.onpointerup = cv.onpointercancel = e=>{
    ptrs.delete(e.pointerId);
    if(!ptrs.size){
      if(drag && drag.type === 'pan' && drag.dist < 6 && ED.sel){ ED.sel = null; edSyncUI(); edDraw(); }   // tap on empty space: deselect, hide the border
      drag = null; if(moved) edSyncHistory();
    } else if(drag && drag.type==='pinch' && ptrs.size < 2) drag = null;
  };
  cv.addEventListener('wheel', e=>{ e.preventDefault(); const p = pos(e); edZoomAt(e.deltaY < 0 ? 1.12 : 1/1.12, p.x, p.y); }, { passive:false });
  cv.oncontextmenu = e=>e.preventDefault();
}

/* ---------- open / close ---------- */
function edInit(){
  if(ED.ready) return; ED.ready = true; ED.cv = document.getElementById('edCanvas'); ED.g = ED.cv.getContext('2d');
  edSetupPointer(); new ResizeObserver(()=>edResize()).observe(document.getElementById('edStage'));
}
function edSetModelName(value){ ED.name = String(value == null ? '' : value).slice(0,40); }
// v318: the two closed-eye lids must sit UNDER the two open eyes in the layer stack (back -> front), never on top
// of them. New models get this from SHEET_DEFAULT_ORDER_SIDE; this also repairs models saved with the old order.
function edFixFaceKitEyeOrder(order){
  if(!Array.isArray(order)) return order;
  // The inner mouth must always sit behind the complete head silhouette. Older saved layer
  // orders sometimes placed it after the head, which exposed the oversized tongue/throat below
  // the chin whenever a hollow laughing/surprised head was active.
  if(ED.modelType==='animal' && ED.arts && ED.arts.mouth_inner){
    const oldIndex=order.indexOf('mouth_inner');
    if(oldIndex>=0) order.splice(oldIndex,1);
    const headIndex=order.indexOf('head');
    if(headIndex>=0) order.splice(headIndex,0,'mouth_inner');
    else order.unshift('mouth_inner');
  }
  if(ED.modelType==='bird'){
    const bci=order.indexOf('bird_eye_closed'), boi=order.indexOf('bird_eye');
    if(bci>=0 && boi>=0 && bci>boi){ order.splice(bci,1); order.splice(order.indexOf('bird_eye'),0,'bird_eye_closed'); }
  }
  ['L','R'].forEach(side=>{
    const closed='eye_closed_'+side, open='eye_'+side;
    const ci=order.indexOf(closed), oi=order.indexOf(open);
    if(ci>=0 && oi>=0 && ci>oi){
      order.splice(ci,1);
      order.splice(order.indexOf(open),0,closed);
    }
  });
  return order;
}
function edOpen(o){
  if(isPlaying && typeof pausePlayback === 'function') pausePlayback();
  edInit();
  edFillIcons();
  ED.arts = o.arts; ED.assign = {}; SHEET_KEYS.forEach(k=>{ if(o.arts[k]) ED.assign[k] = k; });
  ED.view = o.view === 'side' || o.view === 'back' ? o.view : 'front';
  ED.facing = o.facing === -1 ? -1 : 1;
  ED.modelType = o.modelType || (o.sheet && o.sheet.modelType) || 'humanoid';
  ED.beast = !!(o.beast || (o.sheet && o.sheet.beast));
  ED.dress = !!(o.dress || (o.sheet && o.sheet.dress));
  if(ED.dress && o.arts && o.arts.torso) o.arts.torso.dress = true;
  ED.extraVariants = JSON.parse(JSON.stringify((o.sheet && o.sheet.customVariants) || {headParts:[],expressions:[],gestures:[]}));
  if(!Array.isArray(ED.extraVariants.headParts)) ED.extraVariants.headParts=[];
  if(!Array.isArray(ED.extraVariants.expressions)) ED.extraVariants.expressions=[];
  if(!Array.isArray(ED.extraVariants.gestures)) ED.extraVariants.gestures=[];
  ED.pendingVariantType = null;
  ED.st = o.st;
  if(o.sheet && Number(o.sheet.v||0)<7) normalizeLegacySheetMouthLayout(o.sheet.parts,o.sheet.draw,ED.st);
  ED.order = edFixFaceKitEyeOrder(sheetDepthOrderForView(o.order.slice(),ED.modelType,ED.view)); ED.kind = o.kind; ED.editIdx = o.editIdx == null ? null : o.editIdx; ED.name = o.name || 'Model';
  const nameField=document.getElementById('edModelNameInput'); if(nameField) nameField.value=ED.name;
  ED.lockedParts = {}; if(o.sheet && o.sheet.edit && Array.isArray(o.sheet.edit.locked)) o.sheet.edit.locked.forEach(k=>{ if(o.st && o.st[k]) ED.lockedParts[k] = true; });
  ED.init = edSnap(); ED.undo = []; ED.redo = []; ED.sel = 'torso'; ED.mode = 'size'; ED.bones = true; ED.lock = true; ED.assetId = o.assetId || null; ED.returnLib = !!o.returnLib;
  document.getElementById('sheetEd').classList.add('active'); ED.open = true; ED.needFit = true;
  edSyncUI(); edRenderExtraParts();
  requestAnimationFrame(()=>requestAnimationFrame(()=>{ edResize(); }));
}
function edExtraCategoryLabel(category){ return category==='headParts' ? 'Head / mouth' : category==='expressions' ? 'Face expression' : 'Hand gesture'; }
function edVariantRemoveFlatBackground(canvas){
  const w=canvas.width,h=canvas.height,n=w*h,ctx=canvas.getContext('2d',{willReadFrequently:true});
  let im; try{ im=ctx.getImageData(0,0,w,h); }catch(e){ return; }
  const d=im.data; let transparent=0;
  for(let i=3;i<d.length;i+=4) if(d[i]<200) transparent++;
  if(transparent>=n*0.01) return; // preserve genuine transparent PNG/WebP alpha
  const samples=[],step=Math.max(1,Math.floor((2*w+2*h)/500));
  for(let x=0;x<w;x+=step){ samples.push((x)*4,((h-1)*w+x)*4); }
  for(let y=0;y<h;y+=step){ samples.push((y*w)*4,(y*w+w-1)*4); }
  const median=k=>{const a=samples.map(i=>d[i+k]).sort((a,b)=>a-b);return a[Math.floor(a.length/2)]||0;};
  const bg=[0,1,2].map(median);
  const near=p=>Math.abs(d[p*4]-bg[0])<27&&Math.abs(d[p*4+1]-bg[1])<27&&Math.abs(d[p*4+2]-bg[2])<27;
  const seen=new Uint8Array(n),queue=new Int32Array(n);let head=0,tail=0;
  const push=p=>{if(p>=0&&p<n&&!seen[p]&&d[p*4+3]>20&&near(p)){seen[p]=1;queue[tail++]=p;}};
  for(let x=0;x<w;x++){push(x);push((h-1)*w+x);}
  for(let y=0;y<h;y++){push(y*w);push(y*w+w-1);}
  while(head<tail){const q=queue[head++],x=q%w;d[q*4+3]=0;if(x>0)push(q-1);if(x<w-1)push(q+1);if(q>=w)push(q-w);if(q<n-w)push(q+w);}
  ctx.putImageData(im,0,0);
}
function edPickExtra(kind){
  if(!ED.open) return;
  if(ED.modelType!=='humanoid'){ showToast('Optional human face and hand parts are only for humanoid models', {duration:2200}); return; }
  let category=kind, subkind='';
  if(kind==='head'){
    const choice=window.prompt('Choose the head part type:\n1 = Open-mouth overlay (talking)\n2 = Closed-mouth overlay\n3 = Blink / closed-eyes full head','1');
    if(choice===null) return;
    const map={'1':'mouth-open','2':'mouth-closed','3':'blink-head','open':'mouth-open','closed':'mouth-closed','blink':'blink-head'};
    subkind=map[String(choice).trim().toLowerCase()];
    if(!subkind){ showToast('Choose 1, 2 or 3', {duration:1600}); return; }
    category='headParts';
  } else if(kind==='expression') category='expressions';
  else if(kind==='gesture') category='gestures';
  ED.pendingVariantType={category,kind:subkind};
  const input=document.getElementById('edExtraFileInput'); input.value=''; input.click();
}
function edHandleExtraFile(input){
  const file=input.files && input.files[0]; input.value=''; if(!file || !ED.pendingVariantType) return;
  const pending=ED.pendingVariantType; ED.pendingVariantType=null;
  const reader=new FileReader();
  reader.onload=()=>{
    const img=new Image(); img.onload=()=>{
      const max=640, scale=Math.min(1,max/Math.max(img.naturalWidth,img.naturalHeight));
      const cv=document.createElement('canvas'); cv.width=Math.max(1,Math.round(img.naturalWidth*scale)); cv.height=Math.max(1,Math.round(img.naturalHeight*scale));
      const cx=cv.getContext('2d'); cx.clearRect(0,0,cv.width,cv.height); cx.drawImage(img,0,0,cv.width,cv.height);
      // If the image service returned a flat opaque backdrop rather than real alpha, remove only
      // the edge-connected background pixels. Wide margins in the prompt keep the artwork safe.
      edVariantRemoveFlatBackground(cv);
      const src=cv.toDataURL('image/png');
      const category=pending.category, kind=pending.kind || '';
      const defaultName=(kind==='mouth-open'?'Mouth open':kind==='mouth-closed'?'Mouth closed':kind==='blink-head'?'Blink head':category==='expressions'?'Face expression':'Right-hand gesture');
      let name=(file.name||defaultName).replace(/\.[^.]+$/,'').replace(/[_-]+/g,' ').trim();
      name=window.prompt('Give this part a name',name||defaultName);
      if(name===null) name=defaultName;
      name=String(name||defaultName).trim().slice(0,42)||defaultName;
      const item={id:'extra_'+Date.now().toString(36)+'_'+Math.random().toString(36).slice(2,7),name,kind,src,w:cv.width,h:cv.height,createdAt:Date.now()};
      ED.extraVariants[category].push(item);
      edRenderExtraParts();
      showToast('Added '+name, {duration:1500});
    };
    img.onerror=()=>showToast('Could not read that image', {duration:1800}); img.src=reader.result;
  };
  reader.onerror=()=>showToast('Could not read that file', {duration:1800}); reader.readAsDataURL(file);
}
function edRemoveExtraVariant(category,id){
  if(!ED.extraVariants || !Array.isArray(ED.extraVariants[category])) return;
  ED.extraVariants[category]=ED.extraVariants[category].filter(v=>v.id!==id); edRenderExtraParts();
}
function edRenderExtraParts(){
  const count=document.getElementById('edExtraCount'); if(!count||!ED.extraVariants) return;
  const defs=[
    {key:'headParts',target:'edExtraHeadParts',count:'edExtraHeadCount',pick:'head',label:'Add head part'},
    {key:'expressions',target:'edExtraExpressions',count:'edExtraExpressionCount',pick:'expression',label:'Add expression'},
    {key:'gestures',target:'edExtraGestures',count:'edExtraGestureCount',pick:'gesture',label:'Add gesture'}
  ];
  const allCount=defs.reduce((n,d)=>n+(Array.isArray(ED.extraVariants[d.key])?ED.extraVariants[d.key].length:0),0);
  count.textContent=allCount+' added';
  defs.forEach(d=>{
    const row=document.getElementById(d.target), rowCount=document.getElementById(d.count);
    if(!row) return;
    const items=Array.isArray(ED.extraVariants[d.key])?ED.extraVariants[d.key]:[];
    if(rowCount) rowCount.textContent=items.length+' '+(items.length===1?'part':'parts');
    const cards=items.map(v=>{
      const name=escapeHtml(v.name||'Extra part');
      const detail=v.kind?escapeHtml(v.kind.replace(/-/g,' ')):escapeHtml(edExtraCategoryLabel(d.key));
      const id=String(v.id||'').replace(/[^a-zA-Z0-9_-]/g,'');
      return '<div class="ed-extra-card"><button class="ed-extra-remove" type="button" title="Remove" aria-label="Remove '+name+'" onclick="edRemoveExtraVariant(\''+d.key+'\',\''+id+'\')">×</button><img src="'+v.src+'" alt=""><b title="'+name+'">'+name+'</b><small title="'+detail+'">'+detail+'</small></div>';
    }).join('');
    const add='<button type="button" class="ed-extra-add-card" onclick="edPickExtra(\''+d.pick+'\')" aria-label="'+d.label+'"><span class="plus">+</span><span> Add </span></button>';
    row.innerHTML=cards+add;
  });
}
function edClose(){ ED.open = false; document.getElementById('sheetEd').classList.remove('active'); if(ED.returnLib){ ED.returnLib = false; openModelLibrary(); } }
let edCancelArmed = 0;
function edCancel(){
  if(ED.undo.length && Date.now() > edCancelArmed){ edCancelArmed = Date.now() + 2500; showToast('Tap close again to discard changes', { icon:toastIcons.info, duration:2200 }); return; }
  edCancelArmed = 0; edClose();
}
function buildFromSheet(img, fname, modelType){
  const type = modelType || activeSheetType || 'humanoid';
  const r = sheetParse(img, type);
  if(r.error){
    if(type === 'humanoid' && r.fallbackAutoRig && r.cleanedCanvas && autoRigState){
      const detected = runAutoRigDetection(r.cleanedCanvas);
      if(!detected.error){
        Object.assign(autoRigState, detected);
        autoRigState.modelType = 'humanoid';
        autoRigState.originalJoints = JSON.parse(JSON.stringify(detected.joints));
        autoRigState.warnings = (detected.warnings || []).concat([
          'The upload contains touching cut-out parts, so it opened in whole-image auto-rig mode. The preview provides 17 draggable joint handles; move the blue points onto the shoulders, elbows, wrists, hips, knees and feet before creating the rig.'
        ]);
        closeAutoRigUploader();
        openAutoRigPreview();
        showToast('Only '+r.detectedRegions+' separate regions were found. Opened the 17-joint rig preview.', { icon:toastIcons.info, duration:2600 });
        return;
      }
    }
    showToast(r.error, { icon:toastIcons.info, duration:3000 });
    document.getElementById('autoRigUploadPreviewImg').style.display = 'none';
    document.getElementById('autoRigUploadIcon').style.display = '';
    setUploadSheetType(type);
    document.getElementById('autoRigUploadPreviewImg').style.display = 'none';
    document.getElementById('autoRigUploadIcon').style.display = '';
    return;
  }
  closeAutoRigUploader();
  // Anatomy-position 12-part and current 14-part humanoid sheets are upright/front-facing. Legacy/species sheets keep their side-view convention.
  const view = (type === 'humanoid' && (r.layout === 'anatomy12' || r.layout === 'anatomy14' || r.layout === 'base14')) || r.layout==='winged16' ? 'front' : 'side', side = view === 'side';
  const createKind = sheetCreateMode === 'newAsset' ? 'newAsset' : 'new';
  const directOrder=isWingedHumanoidType(type)?SHEET_DEFAULT_ORDER_WINGED:(side?SHEET_DEFAULT_ORDER_SIDE:SHEET_DEFAULT_ORDER);
  edOpen({ kind:createKind, view, modelType:type, arts:r.arts, st:edDefaultLayout(r.arts, view, type), order:directOrder.filter(k=>r.arts[k]), name:(fname||'Model').replace(/\.[^.]+$/,'').slice(0,24) });
}
function edShowLoading(message){
  const overlay=document.getElementById('edLoadingOverlay');
  const title=document.getElementById('edLoadingTitle');
  if(title)title.textContent=message||'Opening character editor';
  if(overlay){overlay.classList.add('active');overlay.setAttribute('aria-hidden','false');}
}
function edHideLoading(){
  const overlay=document.getElementById('edLoadingOverlay');
  if(overlay){overlay.classList.remove('active');overlay.setAttribute('aria-hidden','true');}
}
function edHideLoadingAfterPaint(){
  requestAnimationFrame(()=>requestAnimationFrame(edHideLoading));
}
// Rebuilds the 10 editable "arts" from a saved sheet (char.sheet / asset.sheet). Resolves to null if any part image fails to load.
function edLoadSheetArts(sh){
  const arts = {}, keys = Object.keys(sh.parts);
  // v387: never hang. A cross-origin image drawn on a canvas used to throw inside onload, so the promise never
  // resolved and the "Preparing model editor" overlay spun forever. Images are now requested with CORS, errors are
  // caught, and every part has a time limit.
  return Promise.all(keys.map(k=>new Promise(res=>{
    const p = sh.parts[k], im = new Image();
    let done = false;
    const finish = ()=>{ if(done) return; done = true; clearTimeout(timer); res(); };
    const timer = setTimeout(finish, 20000);
    im.onload = ()=>{
      try{
        const cv = document.createElement('canvas'); cv.width = p.w; cv.height = p.h; cv.getContext('2d').drawImage(im,0,0);
        const a = edMakeArt(cv, k, sh.modelType || 'humanoid'); a.dpx = p.dpx; a.dpy = p.dpy; a.src = null; arts[k] = a;
      }catch(err){ console.warn('Ethvelle: model part could not be prepared', k, err); }
      finish();
    };
    im.onerror = finish;
    if(/^https?:/i.test(String(p.src||''))) im.crossOrigin = 'anonymous';
    im.src = p.src;
  }))).then(()=> keys.some(k=>!arts[k]) ? null : arts);
}
// Re-open an already-built model (Model tab > Edit rig).
function edOpenFromChar(idx){
  const c=characters[idx];if(!c||!c.sheet)return;
  edShowLoading('Opening character editor');
  let opened=false;
  return edLoadSheetArts(c.sheet).then(arts=>{
    if(!arts){showToast("Couldn't load this model's parts",{icon:toastIcons.info});return;}
    edOpen({kind:'edit',editIdx:idx,view:c.sheet.view,facing:c.sheet.facing,modelType:c.modelType||c.sheet.modelType||'humanoid',sheet:c.sheet,arts,st:JSON.parse(JSON.stringify(c.sheet.edit.st)),order:c.sheet.edit.order,name:c.name});
    opened=true;edHideLoadingAfterPaint();
  }).catch(err=>{
    console.error('Ethvelle: character editor could not be opened',err);
    showToast("Couldn't open this character editor",{icon:toastIcons.info});
  }).finally(()=>{if(!opened)edHideLoading();});
}
// UPDATE: "My models" > pencil now opens the FULL editor (same screen as when the model was first added) for that saved model.
// Rename is its own button next to it. Done saves the changes back into the saved model (and any copy of it on stage).
function edOpenFromAsset(id,returnLib){
  let a=modelAssets.find(x=>String(x.id)===String(id));
  if(!a){
    const published=Array.isArray(publishedModelAssets)?publishedModelAssets.find(x=>String(x.id)===String(id)):null;
    if(published){ a=JSON.parse(JSON.stringify(published)); delete a.origin; modelAssets.push(a); saveModelAssetsToStorage(); }
  }
  if(!a)return;
  if(!a.sheet||!a.sheet.parts||!a.sheet.edit){showToast("This older model can't open in the editor. Rename it here, or upload its part sheet again",{icon:toastIcons.info,duration:2800});openRenameModelModal(id);return;}
  edShowLoading('Preparing model editor');
  let opened=false;
  return edLoadSheetArts(a.sheet).then(arts=>{
    if(!arts){showToast("Couldn't load this model's parts",{icon:toastIcons.info});return;}
    closeModelLibrary();
    edOpen({kind:'asset',assetId:id,returnLib:!!returnLib,view:a.sheet.view,facing:a.sheet.facing,modelType:a.modelType||a.sheet.modelType||'humanoid',sheet:a.sheet,arts,st:JSON.parse(JSON.stringify(a.sheet.edit.st)),order:a.sheet.edit.order,name:a.name});
    opened=true;edHideLoadingAfterPaint();
  }).catch(err=>{
    console.error('Ethvelle: saved-model editor could not be opened',err);
    showToast("Couldn't open this model editor",{icon:toastIcons.info});
  }).finally(()=>{if(!opened)edHideLoading();});
}


/* v260 fix: Done preserves authored body-part attachment offsets; never auto-reset ax/ay before compile. */
/* ---------- 3) Done: layout -> rig + puppet ---------- */
function edCompile(){
  // Front-facing rigs are displayed in a temporary T-pose while editing. Compile the neutral/rest
  // pose instead, so Done never saves that visual stance as the character's default project pose.
  const W = edWorld(true), st = ED.st, P = (k,lx,ly)=>edL2Wbone(W,k,lx,ly), pv = k=>({ x:W[k].x, y:W[k].y }), mix = (a,b,t)=>({ x:a.x+(b.x-a.x)*t, y:a.y+(b.y-a.y)*t });
  const J = { pelvis:pv('torso'), neck:pv('head'), headTop:P('head', st.head.pvx, 0) };
  J.chest = mix(J.pelvis, J.neck, 0.86);
  if(isWingedHumanoidType(ED.modelType)&&st.wingL&&st.wingR){
    J.wingRootL=pv('wingL');J.wingTipL=P('wingL',0,st.wingL.pvy);
    J.wingRootR=pv('wingR');J.wingTipR=P('wingR',edArt('wingR').w,st.wingR.pvy);
  }
  if(isMythicModelType(ED.modelType)&&st.tail&&st.tail_mid&&st.tail_tip){const fin=edArt('tail_tip'),tl=edTailTipLocal(fin);J.tailBase=pv('tail');J.tailJoint1=pv('tail_mid');J.tailJoint2=pv('tail_tip');J.tailTip=P('tail_tip',tl.x,tl.y);}
  else if(st.tail && edArt('tail')){ const tl=edTailTipLocal(edArt('tail')); J.tailBase=pv('tail'); J.tailTip=P('tail',tl.x,tl.y); }
  const birdCompact=ED.modelType==='bird'&&['L','R'].every(side=>st['arm'+side+'_upper']&&st['leg'+side+'_upper'])&& !st.armL_lower&&!st.armR_lower&&!st.legL_lower&&!st.legR_lower;
  const endOf=k=>P(k,st[k].pvx,edArt(k).h);
  ['L','R'].forEach(S=>{
    const up='arm'+S+'_upper',lo='arm'+S+'_lower',ha='hand'+S,th='leg'+S+'_upper',sh='leg'+S+'_lower';
    J['shoulder'+S]=pv(up);
    if(isMythicModelType(ED.modelType)){
      const side=S==='L'?-1:1,base=J.tailBase||pv('torso'),j1=J.tailJoint1||base,j2=J.tailJoint2||j1,tip=J.tailTip||j2;
      J['hip'+S]={x:base.x+side*2,y:base.y};J['knee'+S]={x:j1.x+side*2,y:j1.y};J['ankle'+S]={x:j2.x+side*2,y:j2.y};J['foot'+S]={x:tip.x+side*2,y:tip.y};
    }else J['hip'+S]=pv(th);
    if(st[lo]){
      J['elbow'+S]=pv(lo);
      J['wrist'+S]=st[ha]?pv(ha):mix(J['elbow'+S],endOf(lo),.85);
      J['hand'+S]=st[ha]?P(ha,st[ha].pvx,edArt(ha).h):endOf(lo);
    }else if(birdCompact){
      const tip=endOf(up);J['elbow'+S]=mix(J['shoulder'+S],tip,.78);J['wrist'+S]=mix(J['shoulder'+S],tip,.92);J['hand'+S]=tip;
    }else{
      const tip=endOf(up);J['elbow'+S]=mix(J['shoulder'+S],tip,.8);J['wrist'+S]=mix(J['shoulder'+S],tip,.94);J['hand'+S]=tip;
    }
    if(isMythicModelType(ED.modelType)){/* no leg artwork: the virtual anchors above keep the shared joint schema complete */}
    else if(st[sh]){
      J['knee'+S]=pv(sh);
      if(st['shoe'+S]){J['ankle'+S]=pv('shoe'+S);J['foot'+S]=P('shoe'+S,st['shoe'+S].pvx,edArt('shoe'+S).h);}
      else{J['foot'+S]=endOf(sh);J['ankle'+S]=mix(J['knee'+S],J['foot'+S],.9);}
    }else if(birdCompact){
      const tip=endOf(th);J['knee'+S]=mix(J['hip'+S],tip,.78);J['ankle'+S]=mix(J['hip'+S],tip,.93);J['foot'+S]=tip;
    }else{
      const tip=endOf(th);J['knee'+S]=mix(J['hip'+S],tip,.8);J['ankle'+S]=mix(J['hip'+S],tip,.94);J['foot'+S]=tip;
    }
  });
  const ang = (a,b)=>Math.atan2(b.y-a.y, b.x-a.x), ra = { head:ang(J.neck,J.headTop), torso:ang(J.pelvis,J.neck) };
  if(J.wingRootL&&J.wingTipL){ra.wingL=ang(J.wingRootL,J.wingTipL);ra.wingR=ang(J.wingRootR,J.wingTipR);}
  if(J.tailBase && J.tailTip){if(isMythicModelType(ED.modelType)){ra.tail=ang(J.tailBase,J.tailJoint1);ra.tail_mid=ang(J.tailJoint1,J.tailJoint2);ra.tail_tip=ang(J.tailJoint2,J.tailTip);}else ra.tail=ang(J.tailBase,J.tailTip);}
  ['L','R'].forEach(S=>{
    ra['arm'+S+'_upper'] = ang(J['shoulder'+S],J['elbow'+S]); ra['arm'+S+'_lower'] = ang(J['elbow'+S],J['wrist'+S]); ra['hand'+S] = ang(J['wrist'+S],J['hand'+S]);
    ra['leg'+S+'_upper'] = ang(J['hip'+S],J['knee'+S]);
    ra['leg'+S+'_lower'] = st['shoe'+S] ? ang(J['knee'+S],J['ankle'+S]) : ang(J['knee'+S],J['foot'+S]);
    if(st['shoe'+S]) ra['shoe'+S] = ang(J['ankle'+S],J['foot'+S]);
  });
  // The optional expression/gesture parts ride the exact same bone as their base part (see SHEET_BONES), so
  // they inherit that part's rest angle rather than needing one of their own.
  ra.mouth_closed = ra.head; Object.keys(SHEET_ALT_OF).forEach(a=>{ ra[a] = ra[SHEET_ALT_OF[a]]; });
  let x0 = 1e9, y0 = 1e9, x1 = -1e9, y1 = -1e9;
  ED.order.forEach(k=>{ const a = edArt(k); if(!a) return; [[0,0],[a.w,0],[a.w,a.h],[0,a.h]].forEach(([lx,ly])=>{ const p = P(k,lx,ly); x0 = Math.min(x0,p.x); x1 = Math.max(x1,p.x); y0 = Math.min(y0,p.y); y1 = Math.max(y1,p.y); }); });
  const E = Math.max(1, y1-y0), cx = (x0+x1)/2, cy = (y0+y1)/2;
  const all = Object.values(J), mnX = Math.min(...all.map(p=>p.x)), mxX = Math.max(...all.map(p=>p.x)), mnY = Math.min(...all.map(p=>p.y)), mxY = Math.max(...all.map(p=>p.y));
  const pd = 0.1*Math.max(1, mxY-mnY), Wv = Math.max(1,mxX-mnX)+2*pd, Hv = Math.max(1,mxY-mnY)+2*pd, F = {};
  Object.keys(J).forEach(k=>{ F[k] = { x:(J[k].x-mnX+pd)/Wv, y:(J[k].y-mnY+pd)/Hv }; });
  const rigOverride = sheetBuildRigOverrideFromJoints(F, Wv, Hv, ED.modelType), Hj = Math.max(1, Math.max(J.footL.y,J.footR.y)-J.headTop.y);
  rigOverride.figFrac = Math.max(0.2, Math.min(1.2, SHEET_FILL*Hj/E));
  const parts = {}, draw = {};
  ED.order.forEach(k=>{
    const a = edArt(k); if(!a) return; if(!a.src) a.src = a.canvas.toDataURL('image/png');
    parts[k] = { src:a.src, w:a.w, h:a.h, dpx:a.dpx, dpy:a.dpy, skinTop:a.skinTop, jointColors:a.jointColors||{} };
    draw[k] = { pvx:st[k].pvx, pvy:st[k].pvy, rot:W[k].rot, sx:W[k].sx, sy:W[k].sy, ra:ra[k] };
    // Mouth pieces are a small facial feature positioned BY HAND inside the head's own artwork
    // (st.ax/ay live in the head's pixel space and are draggable in the editor), unlike head_blink
    // or the fist swaps which share their base part's own bone + pivot formula. Store the mouth's
    // offset/rotation/scale relative to the head's pivot so the runtime renderer can nest it inside
    // the head's live transform (see drawSheetCharacter) instead of mis-anchoring it at the head
    // bone's joint the way a same-bone swap would.
    if(SHEET_FACE_NESTED.includes(k)||SHEET_FACE_KIT_NESTED.includes(k)||(typeof SHEET_HUMANOID_FACE_KIT_KEYS!=='undefined'&&SHEET_HUMANOID_FACE_KIT_KEYS.includes(k))||(typeof SHEET_BIRD_FACE_NESTED!=='undefined'&&SHEET_BIRD_FACE_NESTED.includes(k))||k==='beak_open'||k==='mouth_open'||k==='mouth_hold'){
      const hp = st.head;
      draw[k].nestDx = st[k].ax - hp.pvx; draw[k].nestDy = st[k].ay - hp.pvy;
      draw[k].nestRot = (st[k].rot||0)*Math.PI/180;
      draw[k].nestSx = st[k].s*st[k].wx*st[k].fx; draw[k].nestSy = st[k].s*st[k].hy*st[k].fy;
    }
  });
  const th = edArt('head'), tk = 72/Math.max(th.w, th.h), tc = document.createElement('canvas'); tc.width = Math.max(1,Math.round(th.w*tk)); tc.height = Math.max(1,Math.round(th.h*tk)); tc.getContext('2d').drawImage(th.canvas,0,0,tc.width,tc.height);
  const sheet = { v:7, view:ED.view, facing:ED.facing, modelType:ED.modelType || 'humanoid', beast:(ED.modelType==='humanoid'&&!!ED.beast)||undefined, dress:(ED.modelType==='humanoid'&&!!ED.dress)||undefined, parts, draw, order:ED.order.slice(), edit:{ st:JSON.parse(JSON.stringify(st)), order:ED.order.slice(), locked:Object.keys(ED.lockedParts||{}).filter(k=>ED.lockedParts[k]&&st[k]) },
    customVariants:JSON.parse(JSON.stringify(ED.extraVariants || {headParts:[],expressions:[],gestures:[]})),
    refLen:Math.max(1e-3, Math.hypot(J.neck.x-J.pelvis.x, J.neck.y-J.pelvis.y)), H:Hj, rootOff:{ x:J.pelvis.x-cx, y:J.pelvis.y-cy }, thumb:tc.toDataURL('image/png') };
  sheet.rigJoints = JSON.parse(JSON.stringify(F));
  sheet.rigSourceW = Wv; sheet.rigSourceH = Hv;
  sheet.rigOverride = JSON.parse(JSON.stringify(rigOverride));
  return { sheet, rigOverride };
}
// Puts a sheet character's pelvis where it makes the whole figure sit centred on the stage.
function sheetPlace(c){
  if(!c || !c.bones || !c.bones.hip) return;
  // The live site uses the older hip-rooted pose rig. The Lab's sheet rig is reconstructed
  // separately by sheetRigForCharacter(), anchored to this hip so transforms and timeline poses work.
  c.hipRestY = c.bones.hip.y;
}
function applySheetToChar(c, sheet, rigOverride){
  if(!c) return;
  if(!c.restTransform && c.modelTransform) c.restTransform = { ...c.modelTransform };
  c.sheet = JSON.parse(JSON.stringify(sheet || {}));
  try{ sheetPreloadImages(c.sheet); }catch(_e){}
  c.sheet.rigOverride = JSON.parse(JSON.stringify(rigOverride || c.sheet.rigOverride || {}));
  c.rigOverride = undefined; // the Lab's sheet-rig override (21 bones humanoid / 22 species) must never be applied to the legacy rig
  c.thumb = c.sheet.thumb || c.thumb;
  c.parts = {}; c.type = 'custom'; c.modelType = c.sheet.modelType || 'humanoid'; c.skinMesh = null;
  ensureSpeciesTailBone(c);
  if(!c.expr) c.expr = sheetExprFresh();
  sheetPlace(c);
}
// Clear only temporary bone offsets after Done; keep the user's stage position/scale and clips.
// The newly compiled sheet supplies the screenshot's saved default pose, while the legacy rig
// must return to its own base angles so an old drag/animation offset cannot distort that sheet.
function resetCharacterBonesToRest(c){
  if(!c || !c.bones) return;
  const angles = c.baseAngles || {};
  Object.keys(c.bones).forEach(k=>{
    if(c.bones[k] && Number.isFinite(angles[k])) c.bones[k].angle = angles[k];
  });
  if(c.bones.hip && Number.isFinite(c.hipRestY)) c.bones.hip.y = c.hipRestY;
}
function edDone(){
  const nameField=document.getElementById('edModelNameInput');
  ED.name=String(nameField ? nameField.value : ED.name || 'Model').trim().slice(0,40) || 'Model';
  // Animal/bird body-part attachment positions are authored rig data, not a temporary pose.
  // Resetting ax/ay to ED.init here silently discarded every species body-position adjustment.
  // Preserve their exact edited layout. edCompile() uses edWorld(true), which removes only the
  // temporary display stance while retaining attachment positions, rotations, sizing and pivots.
  // Keep the existing humanoid save/reset behavior unchanged; manual reset remains available.
  // FIX: beast-man models (modelType 'humanoid' with the beast flag) are anatomically their
  // own species too  -  same as animal/bird/mythic/winged types  -  and need every joint's authored
  // position/rotation (e.g. a custom neck) to persist. Previously only the tail had a one-off
  // carve-out (v344: "the beast-man tail keeps the rest pose chosen in the editor"); every other
  // beast-man joint still fell through to the plain-humanoid branch below, which silently resets
  // ax/ay/rot back to this session's starting values right before saving  -  so Done would report
  // "Model updated" while quietly discarding the user's structural edits. Plain (non-beast)
  // humanoid models are untouched: they keep the original save/reset behavior exactly as before.
  const preserveSpeciesLayout = ED.modelType === 'animal' || ED.modelType === 'bird' || isMythicModelType(ED.modelType) || isWingedHumanoidType(ED.modelType) || (ED.modelType === 'humanoid' && !!ED.beast) || (ED.modelType === 'humanoid' && !!ED.dress);
  if(!preserveSpeciesLayout && (!edResetSkeletonToDefault(true, true) || !edSkeletonAtDefault(true))){
    showToast('Reset the skeleton to its default position before saving this model', {icon:toastIcons.info, duration:2400});
    return;
  }
  let out;
  try{ out = edCompile(); }
  catch(err){ console.error(err); showToast("Couldn't build this model", {icon:toastIcons.info, duration:2400}); return; }
  const sheet = out.sheet, rigOverride = out.rigOverride;
  sheet.rigOverride = JSON.parse(JSON.stringify(rigOverride));
  const inProjectEditor = !!(document.getElementById('screen-editor') && document.getElementById('screen-editor').classList.contains('active'));
  if(ED.kind === 'asset'){
    const asset = modelAssets.find(x=>x.id === ED.assetId);
    if(!asset){ showToast('That saved model no longer exists', {icon:toastIcons.info}); edClose(); return; }
    asset.name = ED.name;
    asset.sheet = JSON.parse(JSON.stringify(sheet));
    asset.rigOverride = undefined;
    asset.modelType = sheet.modelType || ED.modelType || 'humanoid';
    asset.thumb = sheet.thumb;
    asset.previewThumb = undefined; // rebuild the full-body card image from the newly saved rig
    asset.parts = {};
    asset.updatedAt = Date.now();
    const assetPersisted = saveModelAssetsToStorage();
    characters.forEach(c=>{ if(String(c.assetId) === String(asset.id) && c.type === 'custom'){ c.name=ED.name; applySheetToChar(c, sheet, rigOverride); c.modelType=asset.modelType; resetCharacterBonesToRest(c); } });
    if(inProjectEditor){ syncActiveCharacterRefs(); }
    const returnLib = !!ED.returnLib;
    edClose();
    if(inProjectEditor){ drawStick(); renderCharacterLayers(); syncTransformSliders(); persistProjectState(); }
    if(!returnLib) renderModelsTab();
    showToast(assetPersisted ? 'Model updated' : 'Model updated for this session; storage is full', {duration:2200});
    if(navigator.vibrate) try{ navigator.vibrate(12); }catch(e){}
    return;
  }
  if(ED.kind === 'edit' && characters[ED.editIdx]){
    const c = characters[ED.editIdx];
    c.name = ED.name;
    applySheetToChar(c, sheet, rigOverride); resetCharacterBonesToRest(c); c.modelType = ED.modelType || 'humanoid';
    if(ED.editIdx === activeCharIdx) syncActiveCharacterRefs();
    updateModelAssetFromChar(c);
    edClose();
    if(inProjectEditor){ drawStick(); renderCharacterLayers(); syncTransformSliders(); persistProjectState(); renderTimelineClip(); syncTimelineInnerWidth(); renderRuler(); }
    showToast('Model updated', {duration:1400});
  } else if(ED.kind === 'newAsset'){
    const c = makeCharacter(ED.name);
    applySheetToChar(c, sheet, rigOverride); resetCharacterBonesToRest(c); c.name = ED.name || 'Model'; c.assetId = undefined;
    saveCharacterAsModelAsset(c, {silent:true});
    edClose(); renderModelsTab();
    showToast('Model saved to your library', {duration:1500});
  } else {
    const c = makeCharacter(ED.name);
    applySheetToChar(c, sheet, rigOverride); resetCharacterBonesToRest(c); c.name = ED.name || 'Model'; c.modelType = ED.modelType || 'humanoid';
    c.expr = sheetExprFresh();
    characters.push(c); activeCharIdx = characters.length-1; syncActiveCharacterRefs(); selectedJoint = null;
    saveCharacterAsModelAsset(c, {silent:true});
    edClose();
    if(inProjectEditor){ drawStick(); renderCharacterLayers(); syncTransformSliders(); persistProjectState(); renderTimelineClip(); syncTimelineInnerWidth(); renderRuler(); }
    showToast('Model ready', {duration:1400});
  }
  if(navigator.vibrate) try{ navigator.vibrate(12); }catch(e){}
}



/* ===== ETHVELLE MAIN-SITE BRIDGE FOR THE MODEL LAB'S 21-BONE HUMANOID / 22-BONE SPECIES SHEET RIG ===== */
const ED_ICON_PATHS = {
  x:'<path d="M18 6 6 18M6 6l12 12"/>',
  undo:'<path d="M9 14 4 9l5-5"/><path d="M4 9h10.5a5.5 5.5 0 0 1 0 11H13"/>',
  redo:'<path d="m15 14 5-5-5-5"/><path d="M20 9H9.5a5.5 5.5 0 0 0 0 11H11"/>',
  eye:'<path d="M2 12s3.5-7 10-7 10 7 10 7-3.5 7-10 7S2 12 2 12Z"/><circle cx="12" cy="12" r="3"/>',
  eyeOff:'<path d="m3 3 18 18M10.6 10.6a2 2 0 0 0 2.8 2.8M9.9 5.2A10.6 10.6 0 0 1 12 5c6.5 0 10 7 10 7a17 17 0 0 1-3.3 4.1M6.2 6.2C3.5 8 2 12 2 12s3.5 7 10 7a10 10 0 0 0 4-.8"/>',
  lock:'<rect x="4" y="10" width="16" height="11" rx="2"/><path d="M8 10V7a4 4 0 0 1 8 0v3"/>',
  unlock:'<rect x="4" y="10" width="16" height="11" rx="2"/><path d="M8 10V7a4 4 0 0 1 7.6-1.7"/>',
  flipH:'<path d="M12 3v18M4 7l-3 5 3 5M20 7l3 5-3 5"/>',
  flipV:'<path d="M3 12h18M7 4l5-3 5 3M7 20l5 3 5-3"/>',
  copy:'<rect x="8" y="8" width="12" height="12" rx="2"/><path d="M16 8V5a2 2 0 0 0-2-2H5a2 2 0 0 0-2 2v9a2 2 0 0 0 2 2h3"/>',
  reset:'<path d="M3 12a9 9 0 1 0 3-6.7L3 8"/><path d="M3 3v5h5"/>',
  swap:'<path d="M16 3l4 4-4 4M20 7H4M8 21l-4-4 4-4M4 17h16"/>',
  download:'<path d="M12 3v12M7 10l5 5 5-5"/><path d="M4 17v2a2 2 0 0 0 2 2h12a2 2 0 0 0 2-2v-2"/>'
};
function edIcon(name){ return '<svg viewBox="0 0 24 24" fill="none" stroke="currentColor" stroke-width="1.8" stroke-linecap="round" stroke-linejoin="round">'+(ED_ICON_PATHS[name]||'')+'</svg>'; }
function edFillIcons(){
  document.querySelectorAll('#sheetEd i[data-ic]').forEach(el=>{ el.innerHTML=edIcon(el.dataset.ic); });
}
const SHEET_RIG_BONE_DEFS = [
  {key:'root',parent:null},
  {key:'pelvis',parent:'root',a:'pelvis',b:'pelvisTop'},
  {key:'spine_lower',parent:'pelvis',a:'pelvisTop',b:'spineA'},
  {key:'spine_upper',parent:'spine_lower',a:'spineA',b:'spineB'},
  {key:'chest',parent:'spine_upper',a:'spineB',b:'chest'},
  {key:'neck',parent:'chest',a:'chest',b:'neck'},
  {key:'head',parent:'neck',a:'neck',b:'headTop'},
  {key:'tail',parent:'pelvis',at:0,a:'tailBase',b:'tailTip'}
].concat(['L','R'].flatMap(S=>[
  {key:'clavicle'+S,parent:'chest',a:'chest',b:'shoulder'+S},
  {key:'upperArm'+S,parent:'clavicle'+S,a:'shoulder'+S,b:'elbow'+S},
  {key:'forearm'+S,parent:'upperArm'+S,a:'elbow'+S,b:'wrist'+S},
  {key:'hand'+S,parent:'forearm'+S,a:'wrist'+S,b:'hand'+S},
  {key:'thigh'+S,parent:'pelvis',at:0,a:'hip'+S,b:'knee'+S},
  {key:'shin'+S,parent:'thigh'+S,a:'knee'+S,b:'ankle'+S},
  {key:'foot'+S,parent:'shin'+S,a:'ankle'+S,b:'foot'+S}
]));
function sheetRigDefsForType(modelType){
  if(isMythicModelType(modelType)){
    const omit=new Set(['tail','thighL','thighR','shinL','shinR','footL','footR']);
    return SHEET_RIG_BONE_DEFS.filter(d=>!omit.has(d.key)).concat([
      {key:'tail',parent:'pelvis',at:0,a:'tailBase',b:'tailJoint1'},
      {key:'tail_mid',parent:'tail',a:'tailJoint1',b:'tailJoint2'},
      {key:'tail_tip',parent:'tail_mid',a:'tailJoint2',b:'tailTip'}
    ]);
  }
  if(isWingedHumanoidType(modelType))return SHEET_RIG_BONE_DEFS.filter(d=>d.key!=='tail').concat([
    {key:'wingL',parent:'chest',a:'wingRootL',b:'wingTipL'},
    {key:'wingR',parent:'chest',a:'wingRootR',b:'wingTipR'}
  ]);
  return SHEET_RIG_BONE_DEFS;
}
const SHEET_RIG_JOINT_KEYS = ['headTop','neck','chest','pelvis','tailBase','tailJoint1','tailJoint2','tailTip','wingRootL','wingTipL','wingRootR','wingTipR'].concat(['L','R'].flatMap(S=>['shoulder','elbow','wrist','hand','hip','knee','ankle','foot'].map(n=>n+S)));
const SHEET_RIG_SPINE_SPLIT = [0,.2,.5,.78,1];
const SHEET_RIG_DEFAULT_TEMPLATE = {headTop:[0,.24],neck:[0,.33],chest:[0,.364],pelvis:[0,.5],tailBase:[0,.50],tailJoint1:[0,.64],tailJoint2:[0,.80],tailTip:[0,.95],wingRootL:[-.045,.385],wingTipL:[-.245,.285],shoulderL:[-.035,.364],elbowL:[-.09,.4],wristL:[-.15,.43],handL:[-.19,.45],hipL:[-.03,.505],kneeL:[-.075,.585],ankleL:[-.1,.66],footL:[-.105,.7]};
function sheetRigWithSpineJoints(J){
  const p=J.pelvis, c=J.chest, at=t=>({x:p.x+(c.x-p.x)*t,y:p.y+(c.y-p.y)*t});
  return {...J,pelvisTop:at(SHEET_RIG_SPINE_SPLIT[1]),spineA:at(SHEET_RIG_SPINE_SPLIT[2]),spineB:at(SHEET_RIG_SPINE_SPLIT[3])};
}
function sheetBuildRigOverrideFromJoints(joints, srcW, srcH, modelType){
  const J0={};
  SHEET_RIG_JOINT_KEYS.forEach(k=>{ const p=joints[k]||{x:.5,y:.5}; J0[k]={x:p.x*srcW,y:p.y*srcH}; });
  const J=sheetRigWithSpineJoints(J0), rs={}, lengths={}, angles={}, offsets={};
  const lowerY=isMythicModelType(modelType)?J.tailTip.y:Math.max((J.footL||J.ankleL).y,(J.footR||J.ankleR).y);
  const H=Math.max(1,lowerY-J.headTop.y);
  sheetRigDefsForType(modelType).forEach(d=>{
    if(!d.parent){ rs.root={s:J.pelvis,e:J.pelvis}; return; }
    const A=J[d.a], B=J[d.b], P=rs[d.parent], t=d.at===undefined?1:d.at;
    const dx=B.x-A.x, dy=B.y-A.y;
    lengths[d.key]=Math.max(H*.004,Math.hypot(dx,dy))/H;
    angles[d.key]=Math.atan2(dy,dx);
    offsets[d.key]={x:(A.x-(P.s.x+(P.e.x-P.s.x)*t))/H,y:(A.y-(P.s.y+(P.e.y-P.s.y)*t))/H};
    rs[d.key]={s:A,e:B};
  });
  return {ver:2,lengths,angles,offsets};
}
function sheetRigFromJoints(Jin, modelType){
  const J=sheetRigWithSpineJoints(Jin), bones={}, rs={};
  sheetRigDefsForType(modelType).forEach(d=>{
    if(!d.parent){ bones.root={x:J.pelvis.x,y:J.pelvis.y,angle:0,parent:null,length:0}; rs.root={s:J.pelvis,e:J.pelvis}; return; }
    const A=J[d.a],B=J[d.b],P=rs[d.parent],t=d.at===undefined?1:d.at,dx=B.x-A.x,dy=B.y-A.y;
    const ox=A.x-(P.s.x+(P.e.x-P.s.x)*t),oy=A.y-(P.s.y+(P.e.y-P.s.y)*t);
    const bone={parent:d.parent,length:Math.max(1e-3,Math.hypot(dx,dy)),angle:Math.atan2(dy,dx)};
    if(d.at!==undefined) bone.attachAt=d.at;
    if(Math.abs(ox)>1e-6||Math.abs(oy)>1e-6) bone.off={x:ox,y:oy};
    if(d.key==='head') bone.isHead=true;
    bones[d.key]=bone; rs[d.key]={s:A,e:B};
  });
  return bones;
}
function sheetSolveRig(B, BA){
  const out={};
  const rec=(k,depth)=>{
    if(out[k]) return out[k];
    const b=B&&B[k]; if(!b||depth>40) return null;
    const p=b.parent?rec(b.parent,depth+1):null;
    let sx=b.x||0,sy=b.y||0,D=0;
    if(p){
      const t=b.attachAt===undefined?1:b.attachAt,c=Math.cos(p.D),sn=Math.sin(p.D),ox=b.off?b.off.x:0,oy=b.off?b.off.y:0;
      sx=p.s.x+(p.e.x-p.s.x)*t+ox*c-oy*sn; sy=p.s.y+(p.e.y-p.s.y)*t+ox*sn+oy*c; D=p.D;
    }
    const ang=Number.isFinite(b.angle)?b.angle:0,len=Number.isFinite(b.length)?b.length:0,rest=(BA&&Number.isFinite(BA[k]))?BA[k]:ang,a=ang+D;
    return out[k]={s:{x:sx,y:sy},e:{x:sx+Math.cos(a)*len,y:sy+Math.sin(a)*len},a,D:D+(ang-rest)};
  };
  Object.keys(B||{}).forEach(k=>rec(k,0));
  return out;
}
function ensureSpeciesTailBone(char){
  if(!char || !char.bones) return;
  const type=char.modelType || (char.sheet && char.sheet.modelType) || 'humanoid';
  const beastTail=type==='humanoid' && !!(char.sheet && char.sheet.beast && char.sheet.parts && char.sheet.parts.tail);
  const mythic=isMythicModelType(type), winged=isWingedHumanoidType(type);
  const hasTail=type==='animal'||type==='bird'||beastTail||mythic;
  if(!hasTail&&!winged)return;
  const sh=Number(stageH)||600;
  if(hasTail&&!char.bones.tail){
    char.bones.tail=beastTail||mythic?{parent:'spine',length:sh*(mythic?.14:.075),angle:Math.PI/2,attachAt:mythic?0:.05,attachOffsetX:mythic?0:-sh*.01,attachOffsetY:0}:{parent:'spine',length:sh*.075,angle:Math.PI,attachAt:.62,attachOffsetX:-sh*.025,attachOffsetY:0};
  }
  if(mythic){
    if(!char.bones.tail_mid)char.bones.tail_mid={parent:'tail',length:sh*.12,angle:Math.PI/2,attachAt:1};
    if(!char.bones.tail_tip)char.bones.tail_tip={parent:'tail_mid',length:sh*.10,angle:Math.PI/2,attachAt:1};
  }
  if(winged){
    if(!char.bones.wingL)char.bones.wingL={parent:'spine',length:sh*.14,angle:-2.35,attachAt:.82};
    if(!char.bones.wingR)char.bones.wingR={parent:'spine',length:sh*.14,angle:-.79,attachAt:.82};
  }
  if(!char.baseAngles) char.baseAngles={};
  if(hasTail)['tail','tail_mid','tail_tip'].forEach(k=>{if(char.bones[k]&&!Number.isFinite(char.baseAngles[k]))char.baseAngles[k]=Number.isFinite(char.bones[k].angle)?char.bones[k].angle:Math.PI/2;});
  if(winged)['wingL','wingR'].forEach(k=>{if(char.bones[k]&&!Number.isFinite(char.baseAngles[k]))char.baseAngles[k]=Number.isFinite(char.bones[k].angle)?char.bones[k].angle:0;});
}
function sheetRigForCharacter(char){
  ensureSpeciesTailBone(char);
  const sh=char.sheet, ov=sh.rigOverride||{};
  const modelType=char.modelType || (char.sheet && char.sheet.modelType) || 'humanoid';
  const cx=stageW/2, J={};
  Object.keys(SHEET_RIG_DEFAULT_TEMPLATE).forEach(k=>{
    const t=SHEET_RIG_DEFAULT_TEMPLATE[k]; J[k]={x:cx+t[0]*stageH,y:t[1]*stageH};
    if(/L$/.test(k)) J[k.slice(0,-1)+'R']={x:cx-t[0]*stageH,y:t[1]*stageH};
  });
  const bones=sheetRigFromJoints(J,modelType), baseAngles={};
  // Keep the legacy single tail for animals, birds and beast men; Mythic has its own 3-link chain.
  const beastTail=modelType==='humanoid' && !!(char.sheet && char.sheet.beast && char.sheet.parts && char.sheet.parts.tail);
  if(modelType!=='animal' && modelType!=='bird' && !beastTail && !isMythicModelType(modelType)){delete bones.tail;delete bones.tail_mid;delete bones.tail_tip;}
  const scale=stageH*(Number(ov.figFrac)||.5);
  Object.keys(ov.lengths||{}).forEach(k=>{if(bones[k]&&Number.isFinite(ov.lengths[k]))bones[k].length=Math.max(1e-3,ov.lengths[k]*scale);});
  Object.keys(ov.angles||{}).forEach(k=>{if(bones[k]&&Number.isFinite(ov.angles[k]))bones[k].angle=ov.angles[k];});
  Object.keys(ov.offsets||{}).forEach(k=>{if(bones[k]&&ov.offsets[k]&&Number.isFinite(ov.offsets[k].x)&&Number.isFinite(ov.offsets[k].y))bones[k].off={x:ov.offsets[k].x*scale,y:ov.offsets[k].y*scale};});
  const rootScale=stageH*(Number(ov.figFrac)||.5)/Math.max(1,Number(sh.H)||1);
  const hip=char.bones&&char.bones.hip?char.bones.hip:{x:stageW/2,y:stageH*.5};
  bones.root.x=hip.x+(Number(sh.rootOff&&sh.rootOff.x)||0)*rootScale;
  bones.root.y=hip.y+(Number(sh.rootOff&&sh.rootOff.y)||0)*rootScale;
  Object.keys(bones).forEach(k=>baseAngles[k]=bones[k].angle);
  // Main-site animation keys are absolute legacy angles. Distribute those deltas across the
  // complete 21-bone humanoid / 22-bone species puppet so extra spine, shoulder, neck, foot and tail joints follow.
  const delta=k=>{const b=char.bones&&char.bones[k],r=char.baseAngles&&char.baseAngles[k];return b&&Number.isFinite(b.angle)&&Number.isFinite(r)?b.angle-r:0;};
  const s=delta('spine'),head=delta('head'),legL=delta('legL'),legR=delta('legR');
  const pelvis=(legL+legR)*.025;
  const p={
    pelvis:pelvis,
    spine_lower:s*.4-pelvis, spine_upper:s*.35, chest:s*.25,
    neck:s*.08,
    head:head-s-s*.08,
    tail:delta('tail'), tail_mid:delta('tail_mid'), tail_tip:delta('tail_tip'),
    wingL:delta('wingL'), wingR:delta('wingR')
  };
  ['L','R'].forEach(S=>{
    const a=delta('arm'+S),lo=delta('arm'+S+'_lo'),leg=delta('leg'+S),leglo=delta('leg'+S+'_lo'),hand=delta('hand'+S);
    const clavicle=a*.08-s*.025;
    p['clavicle'+S]=clavicle;
    p['upperArm'+S]=a-s-clavicle;
    p['forearm'+S]=lo-a;
    p['hand'+S]=hand-lo;
    p['thigh'+S]=leg-pelvis;
    p['shin'+S]=leglo-leg;
    // The legacy puppet has no separate toe bone; this small counter-rotation gives the
    // sheet rig's foot/ankle point a natural landing and toe-off response to shin movement.
    p['foot'+S]=-(leglo-leg)*.22;
  });
  Object.keys(bones).forEach(k=>{if(k!=='root')bones[k].angle=baseAngles[k]+(Number(p[k])||0);});
  return {bones,baseAngles};
}

// Main-site upload buttons now always enter the Lab part-sheet creator. The separate legacy
// preview is left available only for previously saved autoRig characters through its edit route.
function openAutoRigUploader(asAsset){ openSheetUploader(!!asAsset, false); }
function startDirectModelUpload(asAsset){ openSheetUploader(!!asAsset, false); }


/* ---------- puppet renderer (used on the animation stage) ---------- */
// Base keys that are a small facial feature positioned BY HAND inside their parent part's own
// artwork (see the nestDx/nestDy comment in edCompile) rather than riding a bone of their own.
// Their alt texture (mouth_open) is already skipped below via SHEET_ALT_OF, so only the base
// key needs to be named here.
const SHEET_FACE_NESTED = ['mouth_closed'];
// The animal 11-part kit is nested into the active head transform so every eye, lid, brow and inner-mouth layer follows the face exactly.
const SHEET_FACE_KIT_NESTED = ['mouth_inner','eye_L','eye_R','eye_closed_L','eye_closed_R','brow_L','brow_R'];
const SHEET_BIRD_FACE_NESTED = ['bird_eye_closed','bird_eye','bird_brow','beak_closed'];
// Returns the compiled transform needed to draw `key` inside its parent part. Fresh model
// saves (edCompile) write the exact runtime transform and pivot into sh.draw[key]; treat that
// compiled record as canonical so canvas rendering uses exactly what was saved at Done.
// The editor snapshot is a migration fallback for older assets that predate compiled nesting.
function sheetNestInfo(sh,key){
  const part=sh&&sh.parts&&sh.parts[key],d=sh&&sh.draw&&sh.draw[key];
  const stx=sh&&sh.edit&&sh.edit.st,hp=stx&&stx.head,kp=stx&&stx[key];
  const partPivotX=Number(part&&part.dpx)||0,partPivotY=Number(part&&part.dpy)||0;
  const drawPivotX=d&&Number.isFinite(Number(d.pvx))?Number(d.pvx):partPivotX;
  const drawPivotY=d&&Number.isFinite(Number(d.pvy))?Number(d.pvy):partPivotY;
  if(d&&Number.isFinite(Number(d.nestDx))&&Number.isFinite(Number(d.nestDy))){
    return {...d,nestPivotX:drawPivotX,nestPivotY:drawPivotY};
  }
  if(hp&&kp){
    const pivotX=Number.isFinite(Number(kp.pvx))?Number(kp.pvx):drawPivotX;
    const pivotY=Number.isFinite(Number(kp.pvy))?Number(kp.pvy):drawPivotY;
    return {
      nestDx:Number(kp.ax)-Number(hp.pvx||0),
      nestDy:Number(kp.ay)-Number(hp.pvy||0),
      nestRot:(Number(kp.rot)||0)*Math.PI/180,
      nestSx:(Number(kp.s)||1)*(Number(kp.wx)||1)*(Number(kp.fx)||1),
      nestSy:(Number(kp.s)||1)*(Number(kp.hy)||1)*(Number(kp.fy)||1),
      nestPivotX:pivotX,nestPivotY:pivotY
    };
  }
  return null;
}

// Build a filled version of the base head's OUTER silhouette. Transparent holes enclosed by
// the head (including the mouth opening) are filled, while transparent pixels connected to the
// canvas edge stay transparent. This lets the inner-mouth layer show through the mouth hole but
// makes it physically impossible for it to spill below/outside the animal's face.
function sheetAnimalHeadSilhouetteMask(sh){
  const head=sh&&sh.parts&&sh.parts.head;
  if(!head||!head.src||!head.w||!head.h) return null;
  const img=sheetImgFor(head.src);
  if(!img.complete||!img.naturalWidth){
    if(!img.__animalSilhouetteHook){
      img.__animalSilhouetteHook=true;
      img.addEventListener('load',()=>{if(typeof drawStick==='function')drawStick();},{once:true});
    }
    return null;
  }
  const cache=window.__ethAnimalHeadSilhouetteCache||(window.__ethAnimalHeadSilhouetteCache=new Map());
  const key=String(head.src)+'|'+head.w+'x'+head.h;
  if(cache.has(key)) return cache.get(key);
  const cv=document.createElement('canvas');cv.width=head.w;cv.height=head.h;
  const ctx=cv.getContext('2d',{willReadFrequently:true});
  if(!ctx) return null;
  ctx.clearRect(0,0,cv.width,cv.height);
  ctx.drawImage(img,0,0,head.w,head.h);
  let im;
  try{im=ctx.getImageData(0,0,cv.width,cv.height);}catch(_e){return null;}
  const d=im.data,w=cv.width,h=cv.height,n=w*h,seen=new Uint8Array(n),queue=new Int32Array(n);
  let qh=0,qt=0;
  const push=p=>{
    if(p<0||p>=n||seen[p]||d[p*4+3]>18)return;
    seen[p]=1;queue[qt++]=p;
  };
  for(let x=0;x<w;x++){push(x);push((h-1)*w+x);}
  for(let y=0;y<h;y++){push(y*w);push(y*w+w-1);}
  while(qh<qt){
    const p=queue[qh++],x=p%w;
    if(x>0)push(p-1);
    if(x<w-1)push(p+1);
    if(p>=w)push(p-w);
    if(p<n-w)push(p+w);
  }
  for(let p=0;p<n;p++){
    const a=p*4+3;
    // Keep the original anti-aliased outside contour. Fill only enclosed transparent holes.
    if(!seen[p] && d[a]===0) d[a]=255;
  }
  ctx.putImageData(im,0,0);
  if(cache.size>24)cache.delete(cache.keys().next().value);
  cache.set(key,cv);
  return cv;
}
