// The inner mouth is authored in head-local coordinates. Pre-compose it once and clip it to
// the filled outer silhouette, so even an intentionally oversized mouth asset cannot appear
// outside the muzzle. The cache is per compiled sheet; edits create a new sheet and new cache key.
function sheetAnimalMouthInnerLayer(sh){
  const mouth=sh&&sh.parts&&sh.parts.mouth_inner,head=sh&&sh.parts&&sh.parts.head;
  const nest=mouth&&sheetNestInfo(sh,'mouth_inner'),hd=sh&&sh.draw&&sh.draw.head;
  if(!mouth||!head||!nest||!hd||!mouth.src) return null;
  const caches=window.__ethAnimalMouthLayerCache||(window.__ethAnimalMouthLayerCache=new WeakMap());
  const headPivotX=Number.isFinite(Number(hd.pvx))?Number(hd.pvx):(Number(head.dpx)||0);
  const headPivotY=Number.isFinite(Number(hd.pvy))?Number(hd.pvy):(Number(head.dpy)||0);
  const mouthPivotX=Number.isFinite(Number(nest.nestPivotX))?Number(nest.nestPivotX):(Number(mouth.dpx)||0);
  const mouthPivotY=Number.isFinite(Number(nest.nestPivotY))?Number(nest.nestPivotY):(Number(mouth.dpy)||0);
  const nums=[nest.nestDx,nest.nestDy,nest.nestRot,nest.nestSx,nest.nestSy,headPivotX,headPivotY,mouthPivotX,mouthPivotY].map(v=>Number(v)||0);
  const cacheKey=[head.src,mouth.src,head.w,head.h,mouth.w,mouth.h,...nums.map(v=>v.toFixed(4))].join('|');
  const prior=caches.get(sh);
  if(prior&&prior.key===cacheKey) return prior.value;
  const mouthImg=sheetImgFor(mouth.src),mask=sheetAnimalHeadSilhouetteMask(sh);
  if(!mouthImg.complete||!mouthImg.naturalWidth||!mask) return null;
  const cv=document.createElement('canvas');cv.width=head.w;cv.height=head.h;
  const ctx=cv.getContext('2d');
  if(!ctx) return null;
  ctx.clearRect(0,0,cv.width,cv.height);
  ctx.translate(headPivotX+nums[0],headPivotY+nums[1]);
  ctx.rotate(nums[2]);
  ctx.scale(nums[3]||1,nums[4]||1);
  ctx.drawImage(mouthImg,-mouthPivotX,-mouthPivotY,mouth.w,mouth.h);
  ctx.setTransform(1,0,0,1,0,0);
  ctx.globalCompositeOperation='destination-in';
  ctx.drawImage(mask,0,0,head.w,head.h);
  ctx.globalCompositeOperation='source-over';
  const value={canvas:cv,pivotX:headPivotX,pivotY:headPivotY};
  caches.set(sh,{key:cacheKey,value});
  return value;
}
// Every ordinary part is a rigid image pinned to its own bone start: translate to the joint, rotate by
// how far the bone has turned from rest, then apply the part's own rest rotation / size / flip from the
// editor. The small facial-feature parts in SHEET_FACE_NESTED skip that and instead ride along inside
// their parent part's own transform (captured as parentXform below), offset by the hand-placed spot the
// editor stored for them -- otherwise they'd be drawn at their parent's BONE joint (e.g. the neck) instead
// of wherever on the face they were actually positioned.
function sheetExtraVariant(sh, category, id){
  const list=sh && sh.customVariants && sh.customVariants[category]; if(!Array.isArray(list)||!list.length) return null;
  return id ? (list.find(v=>v.id===id)||null) : null;
}
function sheetLatestHeadPart(sh, kind){
  const list=sh && sh.customVariants && sh.customVariants.headParts; if(!Array.isArray(list)) return null;
  for(let i=list.length-1;i>=0;i--) if(list[i].kind===kind) return list[i];
  return null;
}
function sheetRuntimeHeadVariant(sh, expr){
  if(!sh || !expr) return null;
  // A mood/custom expression stays as the base head during a blink; the renderer overlays
  // the eyelid pixels only. A neutral face may still use the complete closed-eye head swap.
  if((expr.blink || expr.ambientBlink) && !expr.emo && !expr.customExpressionId){
    const v=sheetLatestHeadPart(sh,'blink-head'); if(v) return v;
  }
  if(expr.customExpressionId){ const v=sheetExtraVariant(sh,'expressions',expr.customExpressionId); if(v) return v; }
  return null;
}
function sheetRuntimeMouthVariant(sh, expr){
  if(!sh || !expr) return null;
  return sheetLatestHeadPart(sh, expr.mouth==='open' ? 'mouth-open' : 'mouth-closed');
}
function sheetRuntimeHandVariant(sh, expr, side){
  if(!sh || !expr) return null;
  const id=side==='L'?expr.customGestureLId:expr.customGestureRId;
  return sheetExtraVariant(sh,'gestures',id);
}
function normalizeLegacySheetMouthLayout(parts, draw, state){
  if(!parts || !draw || !state || !parts.head || !state.head) return false;
  const head=parts.head, headState=state.head;
  let changed=false;
  const near=(value,target,tolerance)=>Number.isFinite(Number(value)) && Math.abs(Number(value)-target)<=tolerance;
  ['mouth_closed','mouth_open','mouth_hold'].forEach(key=>{
    const part=parts[key], mouth=state[key];
    if(!part || !mouth) return;
    const fit=Math.max(.12,Math.min(1,(head.w*.34)/Math.max(1,part.w),(head.h*.18)/Math.max(1,part.h)));
    const oldY=near(mouth.ay,head.h*.72,Math.max(1,head.h*.025));
    const previousDefaultY=near(mouth.ay,head.h*.75,Math.max(1,head.h*.025));
    const priorDefaultY=near(mouth.ay,head.h*.82,Math.max(1,head.h*.025));
    const isOldAutoPlacement=
      near(mouth.ax,head.w*.5,Math.max(1,head.w*.015)) &&
      (oldY || previousDefaultY || priorDefaultY) &&
      (near(mouth.s==null?1:mouth.s,1,.035) || near(mouth.s==null?1:mouth.s,fit,.035)) &&
      near(mouth.wx==null?1:mouth.wx,1,.035) &&
      near(mouth.hy==null?1:mouth.hy,1,.035) &&
      near(mouth.fx==null?1:mouth.fx,1,.035) &&
      near(mouth.fy==null?1:mouth.fy,1,.035) &&
      near(mouth.rot==null?0:mouth.rot,0,.5);
    if(!isOldAutoPlacement) return; // never overwrite a user's hand-adjusted mouth
    mouth.ax=head.w*.5;
    mouth.ay=head.h*.88;
    mouth.s=fit;
    const entry=draw[key]||(draw[key]={pvx:part.dpx||0,pvy:part.dpy||0,rot:0,sx:1,sy:1,ra:0});
    entry.nestDx=mouth.ax-(Number(headState.pvx)||0);
    entry.nestDy=mouth.ay-(Number(headState.pvy)||0);
    entry.nestPivotX=Number.isFinite(Number(mouth.pvx))?Number(mouth.pvx):(Number(part.dpx)||0);
    entry.nestPivotY=Number.isFinite(Number(mouth.pvy))?Number(mouth.pvy):(Number(part.dpy)||0);
    entry.pvx=entry.nestPivotX; entry.pvy=entry.nestPivotY;
    entry.nestRot=(Number(mouth.rot)||0)*Math.PI/180;
    entry.nestSx=(Number(mouth.s)||1)*(Number(mouth.wx)||1)*(Number(mouth.fx)||1);
    entry.nestSy=(Number(mouth.s)||1)*(Number(mouth.hy)||1)*(Number(mouth.fy)||1);
    changed=true;
  });
  return changed;
}
// Animal face kit: the inner mouth (tongue + throat) sits BEHIND the head and is only meant to show through the
// hollow mouth of an emotion head. On the closed-mouth base head it is hidden, so the oversized piece can never
// peek out past the chin. Custom one-off expression heads keep it visible so the artist's own keyframes decide.
function sheetInnerMouthShown(sh, expr, hasDynamicHead){
  if(expr && expr.faceKitHideMouth) return false;
  if(!sh || !sh.parts || !sh.parts.mouth_inner) return true;
  if(!SHEET_EMO_HEADS.some(k=>sh.parts[k])) return true;       // no emotion heads in this model: keep the old behaviour
  if(hasDynamicHead) return true;
  if(!expr) return false;
  if(expr.customExpressionId) return true;
  return SHEET_EMO_HEADS.includes(sheetActiveKey('head', expr, sh.parts));
}
// v318: one image cache shared by every canvas (stage, preview, export). Every part image of a sheet is requested as
// soon as the model is applied / first drawn, so an eye, lid, brow or expression head is never skipped just because
// its image had not been decoded yet (that was one cause of parts vanishing for a moment during animations/export).
function sheetImgFor(src){
  const cache = window.__ethSheetImgCache || (window.__ethSheetImgCache = {});
  let img = cache[src]; if(!img){ img = new Image(); img.src = src; cache[src] = img; }
  return img;
}
function sheetPreloadImages(sh){
  if(!sh || !sh.parts) return;
  const done = window.__ethSheetPreloaded || (window.__ethSheetPreloaded = new WeakSet());
  if(done.has(sh)) return; done.add(sh);
  Object.keys(sh.parts).forEach(pk=>{ const pp = sh.parts[pk]; if(pp && pp.src) sheetImgFor(pp.src); });
}
/* ===== v366 Female (saree / dress) model: reads the girl-dress base sheet, hides the legs under the garment
   and stretches the lower garment with the leg movement. Male / every other model type is untouched. ===== */
function sheetOrderDressAnatomy(parts,w,h,beast){
  const need=beast?15:14;
  if(!parts||parts.length!==need)return null;
  const cx=b=>(b.x0+b.x1)/2,cy=b=>(b.y0+b.y1)/2,bh=b=>b.y1-b.y0+1,bw=b=>b.x1-b.x0+1;
  const body=parts.slice().sort((a,b)=>b.n-a.n)[0];
  if(bh(body)<Math.max(...parts.map(bh))*.9)return null;          // the garment body is the tallest piece
  const rest0=parts.filter(b=>b!==body);
  const inCol=rest0.filter(b=>cx(b)>=body.x0-bw(body)*.1&&cx(b)<=body.x1+bw(body)*.1&&bh(b)<bh(body)*.6).sort((a,b)=>a.y0-b.y0);
  const head=inCol[0]||rest0.slice().sort((a,b)=>a.y0-b.y0)[0];
  if(!head||cy(head)>=cy(body))return null;
  const axis=cx(head),rest=rest0.filter(b=>b!==head);
  const order12=r=>{
    const left=r.filter(b=>cx(b)<axis),right=r.filter(b=>cx(b)>=axis);
    if(left.length!==6||right.length!==6)return null;
    const byY=a=>a.slice().sort((p,q)=>cy(p)-cy(q)),hs=a=>a.reduce((s,b)=>s+bh(b),0);
    const chains=side=>{
      const byD=side.slice().sort((a,b)=>Math.abs(cx(a)-axis)-Math.abs(cx(b)-axis));
      const inner=byD.slice(0,3),outer=byD.slice(3);
      const sep=Math.min(...outer.map(b=>Math.abs(cx(b)-axis)))-Math.max(...inner.map(b=>Math.abs(cx(b)-axis)));
      if(sep>w*.035){ return hs(outer)>hs(inner)*1.12?{arms:byY(inner),legs:byY(outer)}:{arms:byY(outer),legs:byY(inner)}; }
      const ys=byY(side); return {arms:ys.slice(0,3),legs:ys.slice(3)};     // one stack of six: arm pieces on top, leg pieces below
    };
    const L=chains(left),R=chains(right);
    if(hs(L.legs)+hs(R.legs)<(hs(L.arms)+hs(R.arms))*.9)return null;
    return [L.arms[0],R.arms[0],L.arms[1],L.arms[2],R.arms[1],R.arms[2],L.legs[0],R.legs[0],L.legs[1],R.legs[1],L.legs[2],R.legs[2]];
  };
  if(!beast){const l=order12(rest);return l?[head,body,...l]:null;}
  const cands=rest.slice().sort((a,b)=>Math.abs(cx(a)-axis)-Math.abs(cx(b)-axis));
  for(const tail of cands){const l=order12(rest.filter(b=>b!==tail));if(l)return [head,body,tail,...l];}
  return null;
}
// Girl dress body: the pelvis pivot sits on the hip line of the tall garment piece and the neck connector is the centre line.
function edApplyDressPivot(A){
  const T=A&&A.torso;if(!T)return;
  T.dress=true;
  const hh=A.head?A.head.h:T.h/6;
  const legLen=(A.legL_upper?A.legL_upper.h*.86:0)+(A.legL_lower?A.legL_lower.h*.92:0);
  let p=legLen>0?T.h-legLen*.98:0;
  if(!(p>=T.h*.34&&p<=T.h*.60))p=Math.max(T.h*.34,Math.min(T.h*.64,hh*2.25));
  T.dpx=Number.isFinite(T.topCx)?T.topCx:T.w/2;T.dpy=p;
}
// Draws the dress body with its lower half bent by the legs (hem follows knees and feet). g is already in the torso's local pixel space
// (origin = pivot). Returns false when the pose is at rest, so the caller draws the plain image.
function sheetDrawDressBody(g,img,d,rot,k,a,S,B,BA,sh){
  try{
    const iw=img.naturalWidth,ih=img.naturalHeight,kx=k*d.sx,ky=k*d.sy;
    if(!(iw>0&&ih>0)||!(Math.abs(kx)>1e-6)||!(Math.abs(ky)>1e-6)||!S||!B)return false;
    const Br={};Object.keys(B).forEach(n=>{const b=B[n];Br[n]=Object.assign({},b,{angle:(BA&&Number.isFinite(BA[n]))?BA[n]:b.angle});});
    const R=sheetSolveRig(Br,BA);
    if(!R.pelvis||!R.neck)return false;
    const rotR=Math.atan2(R.neck.e.y-R.pelvis.s.y,R.neck.e.x-R.pelvis.s.x)-d.ra+d.rot;
    const loc=(P,o,r)=>{const dx=P.x-o.x,dy=P.y-o.y,c=Math.cos(r),s=Math.sin(r);return {x:(dx*c+dy*s)/kx+d.pvx,y:(-dx*s+dy*c)/ky+d.pvy};};
    const legs=['L','R'].map(sd=>{
      const T1=S['thigh'+sd],T2=S['shin'+sd],T3=S['foot'+sd],R1=R['thigh'+sd],R2=R['shin'+sd],R3=R['foot'+sd];
      if(!T1||!T2||!R1||!R2)return null;
      const pt=(Sx,Rx,end)=>({c:loc(end?Sx.e:Sx.s,a.s,rot),r:loc(end?Rx.e:Rx.s,R.pelvis.s,rotR)});
      const H=pt(T1,R1,false),K=pt(T2,R2,false),A=T3&&R3?pt(T3,R3,false):pt(T2,R2,true);
      // v373: half-width of the leg art, in the garment's own pixels, so the dress can always cover the leg.
      const hwOf=key=>{const pp=sh&&sh.parts&&sh.parts[key],dd=sh&&sh.draw&&sh.draw[key];return pp&&dd&&Number(pp.w)>0&&Math.abs(d.sx)>1e-6?.5*pp.w*Math.abs(dd.sx)/Math.abs(d.sx):0;};
      const hwT=hwOf('leg'+sd+'_upper'),hwS=hwOf('leg'+sd+'_lower')||hwT*.8;
      return {Hy:H.r.y,Ky:K.r.y,Ay:A.r.y,Hx:H.r.x,Kx:K.r.x,Ax:A.r.x,hwT,hwS,dK:{x:K.c.x-K.r.x,y:K.c.y-K.r.y},dA:{x:A.c.x-A.r.x,y:A.c.y-A.r.y}};
    });
    if(!legs[0]||!legs[1])return false;
    if(Math.max(Math.hypot(legs[0].dK.x,legs[0].dK.y),Math.hypot(legs[0].dA.x,legs[0].dA.y),Math.hypot(legs[1].dK.x,legs[1].dK.y),Math.hypot(legs[1].dA.x,legs[1].dA.y))<.6)return false;
    const lf=legs[0].Ax<=legs[1].Ax?legs[0]:legs[1],rt=lf===legs[0]?legs[1]:legs[0];
    const yTop=Math.max(0,Math.min(ih-3,(lf.Hy+rt.Hy)/2));
    // v373 EVEN STRETCH + ALWAYS COVER THE LEGS.
    // Old version: each leg's pull was blended over the narrow gap between the two ankles, so all of the stretching was
    // squeezed into one small area, and nothing checked that the cloth still covered the legs.
    // Now every row of the garment is stretched LINEARLY across the garment's whole width: the left edge follows the left
    // leg, the right edge follows the right leg, and everything in between is spread evenly (the same on the left,
    // the middle and the right). The vertical pull is spread across the width the same way. Then two guards keep the
    // legs inside the cloth: the side edges are never allowed to cut inside a leg, and the hem is never allowed to
    // rise above a foot (both only act when the cloth would otherwise lose a margin it had at rest).
    const clamp01=t=>t<0?0:t>1?1:t,lerp=(p,q,t)=>p+(q-p)*t;
    const ease=clamp01;   // linear along each leg bone = exactly how the leg itself moves, so the cloth can never lag behind it
    const dispOf=(L,y)=>{
      if(y<=L.Hy)return {x:0,y:0};
      if(y<=L.Ky){const t=ease((y-L.Hy)/Math.max(1e-3,L.Ky-L.Hy));return {x:L.dK.x*t,y:L.dK.y*t};}
      if(y<=L.Ay){const t=ease((y-L.Ky)/Math.max(1e-3,L.Ay-L.Ky));return {x:lerp(L.dK.x,L.dA.x,t),y:lerp(L.dK.y,L.dA.y,t)};}
      return L.dA;
    };
    const restX=(L,y)=>y<=L.Ky?lerp(L.Hx,L.Kx,clamp01((y-L.Hy)/Math.max(1e-3,L.Ky-L.Hy))):lerp(L.Kx,L.Ax,clamp01((y-L.Ky)/Math.max(1e-3,L.Ay-L.Ky)));
    const halfW=(L,y)=>y<=L.Ky?lerp(L.hwT,L.hwS,clamp01((y-L.Hy)/Math.max(1e-3,L.Ky-L.Hy))):L.hwS;
    // where the opaque cloth starts and ends on every row (read once from the picture, then cached on it)
    if(!img.__dressExt||img.__dressExt.iw!==iw||img.__dressExt.ih!==ih){
      let L=null,R=null,sc=1;
      try{
        sc=Math.min(1,256/iw);const cw=Math.max(2,Math.round(iw*sc)),ch=Math.max(2,Math.round(ih*sc));
        const cv=document.createElement('canvas');cv.width=cw;cv.height=ch;
        const cg=cv.getContext('2d',{willReadFrequently:true});cg.drawImage(img,0,0,cw,ch);
        const px=cg.getImageData(0,0,cw,ch).data;L=new Float32Array(ch).fill(-1);R=new Float32Array(ch).fill(-1);
        for(let y=0;y<ch;y++){let a0=-1,a1=-1;for(let x=0;x<cw;x++){if(px[(y*cw+x)*4+3]>24){if(a0<0)a0=x;a1=x;}}if(a0>=0){L[y]=a0/sc;R[y]=(a1+1)/sc;}}
        let last=-1;for(let y=0;y<ch;y++){if(L[y]>=0)last=y;else if(last>=0){L[y]=L[last];R[y]=R[last];}}
        let nxt=-1;for(let y=ch-1;y>=0;y--){if(L[y]>=0)nxt=y;else if(nxt>=0){L[y]=L[nxt];R[y]=R[nxt];}}
        for(let y=0;y<ch;y++)if(L[y]<0){L[y]=0;R[y]=iw;}
      }catch(e){L=null;R=null;sc=1;}
      img.__dressExt={iw,ih,L,R,sc};
    }
    const EX=img.__dressExt;
    const extAt=(arr,sy,dflt)=>{if(!arr)return dflt;const f=Math.max(0,Math.min(arr.length-1,sy*EX.sc)),i0=Math.floor(f),i1=Math.min(arr.length-1,i0+1);return lerp(arr[i0],arr[i1],f-i0);};
    const edgesAt=sy=>{let eL=extAt(EX.L,sy,0),eR=extAt(EX.R,sy,iw);if(!(eR-eL>iw*.05)){eL=0;eR=iw;}return {eL,eR};};
    const pad=iw*.012,padY=ih*.01;
    // hem guard: at the bottom row, the cloth above each foot must move down at least as far as that foot does
    const bot=edgesAt(ih-1),extraBot=(()=>{let need=0;[lf,rt].forEach(L=>{
      const u=clamp01((restX(L,ih)-bot.eL)/Math.max(1,bot.eR-bot.eL)),hem=lerp(lf.dA.y,rt.dA.y,u),m=Math.max(0,ih-L.Ay);
      need=Math.max(need,L.dA.y-hem-Math.max(0,m-padY));});return need;})();
    const NX=14,NY=18,rows=[];
    for(let j=0;j<=NY;j++){
      const sy=yTop+(ih-yTop)*j/NY,row=[],dl=dispOf(lf,sy),dr=dispOf(rt,sy),E=edgesAt(sy),W=E.eR-E.eL;
      // side guard: how much spare cloth each leg has beyond it at rest (never less than a small pad is demanded)
      const slackL=L=>Math.max(0,(restX(L,sy)-halfW(L,sy)-E.eL)-pad),slackR=L=>Math.max(0,(E.eR-(restX(L,sy)+halfW(L,sy)))-pad);
      let dLe=Math.min(dl.x,dr.x+slackL(rt)),dRe=Math.max(dr.x,dl.x-slackR(lf));
      dRe=Math.max(dRe,dLe-.7*W);                                   // never fold the row over itself
      const wy=Math.pow(clamp01((sy-yTop)/Math.max(1e-3,ih-yTop)),1.5),ex=Math.max(0,extraBot)*wy;
      for(let i=0;i<=NX;i++){
        const sx=iw*i/NX,u=clamp01((sx-E.eL)/W);
        row.push({sx,sy,x:sx+lerp(dLe,dRe,u),y:sy+lerp(dl.y,dr.y,u)+ex});
      }
      rows.push(row);
    }
    for(let j=1;j<=NY;j++)for(let i=0;i<=NX;i++){const p=rows[j-1][i],q=rows[j][i],mn=(q.sy-p.sy)*.25;if(q.y<p.y+mn)q.y=p.y+mn;}
    for(let j=0;j<=NY;j++)for(let i=1;i<=NX;i++){const p=rows[j][i-1],q=rows[j][i],mn=(q.sx-p.sx)*.25;if(q.x<p.x+mn)q.x=p.x+mn;}
    const ov=.9/Math.max(1e-6,Math.min(Math.abs(kx),Math.abs(ky)));
    g.drawImage(img,0,0,iw,Math.min(ih,Math.ceil(yTop)+1),-d.pvx,-d.pvy,iw,Math.min(ih,Math.ceil(yTop)+1));
    const tri=(s0,s1,s2)=>{
      const den=(s1.sx-s0.sx)*(s2.sy-s0.sy)-(s2.sx-s0.sx)*(s1.sy-s0.sy);if(Math.abs(den)<1e-8)return;
      const d0={x:s0.x-d.pvx,y:s0.y-d.pvy},d1={x:s1.x-d.pvx,y:s1.y-d.pvy},d2={x:s2.x-d.pvx,y:s2.y-d.pvy};
      const A1=((d1.x-d0.x)*(s2.sy-s0.sy)-(d2.x-d0.x)*(s1.sy-s0.sy))/den,B1=((d1.y-d0.y)*(s2.sy-s0.sy)-(d2.y-d0.y)*(s1.sy-s0.sy))/den;
      const C1=((d2.x-d0.x)*(s1.sx-s0.sx)-(d1.x-d0.x)*(s2.sx-s0.sx))/den,D1=((d2.y-d0.y)*(s1.sx-s0.sx)-(d1.y-d0.y)*(s2.sx-s0.sx))/den;
      const E1=d0.x-(A1*s0.sx+C1*s0.sy),F1=d0.y-(B1*s0.sx+D1*s0.sy);
      const ccx=(d0.x+d1.x+d2.x)/3,ccy=(d0.y+d1.y+d2.y)/3,out=p=>{const vx=p.x-ccx,vy=p.y-ccy,l=Math.hypot(vx,vy)||1;return {x:p.x+vx/l*ov,y:p.y+vy/l*ov};};
      const p0=out(d0),p1=out(d1),p2=out(d2);
      const bx=Math.max(0,Math.floor(Math.min(s0.sx,s1.sx,s2.sx))-1),by=Math.max(0,Math.floor(Math.min(s0.sy,s1.sy,s2.sy))-1);
      const bx1=Math.min(iw,Math.ceil(Math.max(s0.sx,s1.sx,s2.sx))+1),by1=Math.min(ih,Math.ceil(Math.max(s0.sy,s1.sy,s2.sy))+1);
      if(bx1<=bx||by1<=by)return;
      g.save();g.beginPath();g.moveTo(p0.x,p0.y);g.lineTo(p1.x,p1.y);g.lineTo(p2.x,p2.y);g.closePath();g.clip();
      g.transform(A1,B1,C1,D1,E1,F1);g.drawImage(img,bx,by,bx1-bx,by1-by,bx,by,bx1-bx,by1-by);g.restore();
    };
    for(let j=0;j<NY;j++)for(let i=0;i<NX;i++){
      const p00=rows[j][i],p10=rows[j][i+1],p01=rows[j+1][i],p11=rows[j+1][i+1];
      tri(p00,p10,p01);tri(p10,p11,p01);
    }
    return true;
  }catch(err){return false;}
}

function drawSheetCharacter(g, char){
  const sh = char.sheet;
  if(!sh || !sh.parts || !sh.draw || !Array.isArray(sh.order)) return;
  if(Number(sh.v||0)<7){
    if(sh.edit && sh.edit.st) normalizeLegacySheetMouthLayout(sh.parts,sh.draw,sh.edit.st);
    sh.v=7;
  }
  const sheetRig = sheetRigForCharacter(char), B = sheetRig.bones, S = sheetSolveRig(B, sheetRig.baseAngles);
  let L = 0; ['pelvis','spine_lower','spine_upper','chest','neck'].forEach(n=>{ if(B[n]) L += B[n].length; });
  const k = L/Math.max(1e-6, sh.refLen);
  if(!g.__sheetImg) g.__sheetImg = {};
  sheetPreloadImages(sh);
  const getImg = sheetImgFor;
  const parentXform = {};
  const modelType=String(char.modelType||(sh&&sh.modelType)||'humanoid');
  const isAnimalFaceKit = modelType==='animal' && SHEET_FACE_KIT_NESTED.some(key=>sh.parts[key]);
  const isHumanoidFaceKit = (modelType==='humanoid' || isMythicModelType(modelType) || isWingedHumanoidType(modelType)) && SHEET_HUMANOID_FACE_KIT_KEYS.some(key=>!!sh.parts[key]);
  const humanoidHasClosedEyes = isHumanoidFaceKit && !!sh.parts.eye_closed_L && !!sh.parts.eye_closed_R;
  const isBirdFaceKit = modelType==='bird' && SHEET_BIRD_FACE_KIT_KEYS.every(key=>!!sh.parts[key]);
  const hasSeparateFaceKit=isAnimalFaceKit||isHumanoidFaceKit||isBirdFaceKit;
  const faceKitDone = {};
  const faceKitExpr = char.expr || {};
  const faceKitEyesClosed = faceKitExpr.eyeState==='closed' || (faceKitExpr.eyeState!=='open' && (faceKitExpr.blink||faceKitExpr.ambientBlink));
  // Normalize z-order at render time too, so older saved characters receive the same
  // arm-depth and tail-over-body fixes without requiring a re-import.
  const renderOrder=sheetDepthOrderForView(sh.order,modelType,sh.view||char.view||'front');
  let faceKitHead = null;
  if(hasSeparateFaceKit){
    const hd=sh.draw&&sh.draw.head,hbn=SHEET_BONES.head,ha=hbn&&S[hbn[0]],hb=hbn&&S[hbn[1]];
    if(hd&&ha&&hb){
      const hAng=Math.atan2(hb.e.y-ha.s.y,hb.e.x-ha.s.x);
      faceKitHead={x:ha.s.x,y:ha.s.y,rot:hAng-(Number(hd.ra)||0)+(Number(hd.rot)||0),sx:k*(Number(hd.sx)||1),sy:k*(Number(hd.sy)||1)};
    }
  }
  const drawFaceKitPart = key=>{
    if(faceKitDone[key]) return; faceKitDone[key]=true;
    const px=faceKitHead||parentXform.head;if(!px)return;
    const sourceKey=(key==='beak_closed')?sheetActiveKey('beak_closed',faceKitExpr,sh.parts):key;
    const part=sh.parts[sourceKey],rawNest=part&&sheetNestInfo(sh,sourceKey);
    const closedBeakNest=sourceKey==='beak_open'?sheetNestInfo(sh,'beak_closed'):null;
    // Open/closed artwork is a direct swap: preserve the saved open-art scale and pivot, but
    // inherit the closed beak's head-local position/rotation so animation cannot make it jump.
    const nest=rawNest&&closedBeakNest?{...rawNest,nestDx:closedBeakNest.nestDx,nestDy:closedBeakNest.nestDy,nestRot:closedBeakNest.nestRot}:rawNest;
    if(!part||!nest||!part.src)return;
    if(sourceKey==='mouth_inner'&&!sheetInnerMouthShown(sh,faceKitExpr,!!sheetRuntimeHeadVariant(sh,faceKitExpr)))return;
    if((sourceKey==='eye_L'||sourceKey==='eye_R'||sourceKey==='bird_eye')&&faceKitEyesClosed&&(isAnimalFaceKit||humanoidHasClosedEyes))return;
    if(sourceKey==='bird_eye'&&faceKitEyesClosed&&isBirdFaceKit&&sh.parts.bird_eye_closed)return;
    if((sourceKey==='eye_closed_L'||sourceKey==='eye_closed_R'||sourceKey==='bird_eye_closed')&&!faceKitEyesClosed)return;
    const img=getImg(part.src);
    if(!img.complete||!img.naturalWidth){if(!img.__hook){img.__hook=true;img.addEventListener('load',()=>{if(typeof drawStick==='function')drawStick();});}return;}
    if(sourceKey==='mouth_inner'){
      const clipped=sheetAnimalMouthInnerLayer(sh);if(!clipped)return;
      g.save();g.translate(px.x,px.y);g.rotate(px.rot);g.scale(px.sx,px.sy);
      g.drawImage(clipped.canvas,-clipped.pivotX,-clipped.pivotY,sh.parts.head.w,sh.parts.head.h);g.restore();return;
    }
    let moodRot=0;
    if(sourceKey==='bird_brow'){
      // A side-profile bird has one visible brow: use a clear up/down tilt, not the tiny
      // mirrored two-brow angles used by humanoids. This keeps each expression readable.
      const emo=faceKitExpr.emo||'';
      if(emo==='angry')moodRot=.30;       // tip slopes down into an angry brow
      else if(emo==='sad')moodRot=-.24;   // inner brow lifts for a worried/sad look
      else if(emo==='laugh')moodRot=.13;
      else if(emo==='shock')moodRot=-.10;
    }else if(sourceKey==='brow_L'||sourceKey==='brow_R'){
      const left=sourceKey==='brow_L',emo=faceKitExpr.emo||'';
      if(emo==='angry')moodRot=(left?1:-1)*.18;
      else if(emo==='sad')moodRot=(left?-1:1)*.11;
      else if(emo==='laugh')moodRot=(left?1:-1)*.07;
      else if(emo==='shock')moodRot=(left?-1:1)*.04;
    }
    const pivotX=Number.isFinite(Number(nest.nestPivotX))?Number(nest.nestPivotX):Number((sh.draw[sourceKey]||{}).pvx??part.dpx)||0;
    const pivotY=Number.isFinite(Number(nest.nestPivotY))?Number(nest.nestPivotY):Number((sh.draw[sourceKey]||{}).pvy??part.dpy)||0;
    g.save();g.translate(px.x,px.y);g.rotate(px.rot);
    g.translate((Number(nest.nestDx)||0)*px.sx,(Number(nest.nestDy)||0)*px.sy);
    g.rotate((Number(nest.nestRot)||0)+moodRot);
    let sx=k*(Number(nest.nestSx)||1),sy=k*(Number(nest.nestSy)||1);
    // Humanoid kits WITHOUT closed-eye parts (legacy 17-part) have no separate closed-eyelid art. Keep their open eyes visible
    // rather than flattening them to 4.5% height during blink/auto-blink. Old 6-part bird kits
    // still use the legacy squash; new 7-part bird kits swap to their dedicated eyelid asset.
    const shouldSquash=faceKitEyesClosed && isBirdFaceKit && sourceKey==='bird_eye' && !sh.parts.bird_eye_closed;
    if(shouldSquash){g.translate(0,(Number(part.h)||img.naturalHeight)*.49);g.scale(1,.045);g.translate(0,-(Number(part.h)||img.naturalHeight)*.49);}
    g.scale(sx,sy);g.drawImage(img,-pivotX,-pivotY,part.w,part.h);g.restore();
  };
  renderOrder.forEach(key=>{
    if(hasSeparateFaceKit&&key==='mouth_inner')return;
    if((isAnimalFaceKit||isHumanoidFaceKit)&&key==='head')drawFaceKitPart('mouth_inner');
    if(isAnimalFaceKit&&SHEET_FACE_KIT_NESTED.includes(key)){drawFaceKitPart(key);return;}
    if(isHumanoidFaceKit&&['eye_L','eye_R','eye_closed_L','eye_closed_R','brow_L','brow_R'].includes(key))return;
    if(isBirdFaceKit&&SHEET_BIRD_FACE_NESTED.includes(key)){drawFaceKitPart(key);return;}
    if(SHEET_ALT_OF[key]||SHEET_FACE_NESTED.includes(key)||(isAnimalFaceKit&&SHEET_FACE_KIT_NESTED.includes(key))||(isHumanoidFaceKit&&SHEET_HUMANOID_FACE_KIT_KEYS.includes(key))||(isBirdFaceKit&&SHEET_BIRD_FACE_NESTED.includes(key)))return;
    if(isAnimalFaceKit&&(key==='eye_L'||key==='eye_R')&&faceKitEyesClosed)return;
    if((key==='eye_L'||key==='eye_R')&&char.expr&&(char.expr.blink||char.expr.ambientBlink||char.expr.eyeState==='closed')&&!hasSeparateFaceKit)return;
    if(key==='mouth_inner'&&!sheetInnerMouthShown(sh,char.expr,!!sheetRuntimeHeadVariant(sh,char.expr)))return;
    const extraHead=key==='head'?sheetRuntimeHeadVariant(sh,char.expr):null;
    const extraHand=key==='handL'||key==='handR'?sheetRuntimeHandVariant(sh,char.expr,key==='handL'?'L':'R'):null;
    const dynamic=extraHead||extraHand;
    const useKey=dynamic?key:sheetActiveKey(key,char.expr,sh.parts);
    const d=sh.draw[useKey],part=dynamic?{src:dynamic.src,w:sh.parts[key].w,h:sh.parts[key].h,dpx:sh.parts[key].dpx,dpy:sh.parts[key].dpy}:sh.parts[useKey];
    let sb=SHEET_BONES[useKey];
    if(useKey==='legL_lower'&&sh.parts.shoeL)sb=['shinL','shinL'];
    if(useKey==='legR_lower'&&sh.parts.shoeR)sb=['shinR','shinR'];
    const a=sb&&S[sb[0]],b=sb&&S[sb[1]];if(!d||!part||!a||!b)return;
    const img=getImg(part.src);
    if(!img.complete||!img.naturalWidth){if(!img.__hook){img.__hook=true;img.addEventListener('load',()=>{if(typeof drawStick==='function')drawStick();});}return;}
    const ang=Math.atan2(b.e.y-a.s.y,b.e.x-a.s.x),rot=ang-d.ra+d.rot;
    if(key==='head')parentXform.head={x:a.s.x,y:a.s.y,rot,sx:k*d.sx,sy:k*d.sy};
    const mirror=extraHand&&key==='handL'&&extraHand.side!=='L'?-1:1;
    g.save();g.translate(a.s.x,a.s.y);g.rotate(rot);g.scale(k*d.sx*mirror,k*d.sy);
    if(dynamic)g.drawImage(img,-d.pvx,-d.pvy,sh.parts[key].w,sh.parts[key].h);
    else if(key==='torso'&&sh.dress&&sheetDrawDressBody(g,img,d,rot,k,a,S,B,sheetRig.baseAngles,sh)){/* drawn as a stretching dress */}
    else g.drawImage(img,-d.pvx,-d.pvy);
    g.restore();
  });
  // Dedicated face kits are painted after the base head; mouth fill is inserted just before that head.
  if(isAnimalFaceKit){SHEET_FACE_KIT_NESTED.forEach(drawFaceKitPart);}
  if(isHumanoidFaceKit){['eye_closed_L','eye_closed_R','eye_L','eye_R','brow_L','brow_R'].forEach(drawFaceKitPart);}
  if(isBirdFaceKit){['bird_eye_closed','bird_eye','bird_brow','beak_closed'].forEach(drawFaceKitPart);}
  // Keep legacy full-head blink eye windows available for older Animal kits only.
  if(isAnimalFaceKit&&char.expr&&(char.expr.blink||char.expr.ambientBlink||char.expr.eyeState==='closed')&&(char.expr.emo||char.expr.customExpressionId)&&parentXform.head){
    const blinkPart=sh.parts.head_blink||sheetLatestHeadPart(sh,'blink-head');
    const headKey=sheetActiveKey('head',{...char.expr,blink:false,ambientBlink:false},sh.parts);
    const headPart=sh.parts[headKey]||sh.parts.head,headDraw=sh.draw[headKey]||sh.draw.head;
    if(blinkPart&&blinkPart.src&&headPart&&headDraw){
      const img=getImg(blinkPart.src);
      if(!img.complete||!img.naturalWidth){if(!img.__hook){img.__hook=true;img.addEventListener('load',()=>{if(typeof drawStick==='function')drawStick();});}}
      else{
        const bw=Number(blinkPart.w)||img.naturalWidth,bh=Number(blinkPart.h)||img.naturalHeight;
        const windows=[{x:.34,y:.565,w:.245,h:.115},{x:.665,y:.565,w:.245,h:.115}],px=parentXform.head;
        g.save();g.translate(px.x,px.y);g.rotate(px.rot);g.scale(px.sx,px.sy);
        windows.forEach(win=>{const sx=bw*win.x,sy=bw*0+bh*win.y,sw=bw*win.w,shh=bh*win.h,dx=-Number(headDraw.pvx||0)+headPart.w*win.x,dy=-Number(headDraw.pvy||0)+headPart.h*win.y;g.drawImage(img,sx,sy,sw,shh,dx,dy,headPart.w*win.w,headPart.h*win.h);});
        g.restore();
      }
    }
  }
  SHEET_FACE_NESTED.forEach(baseKey=>{
    const pKey = SHEET_PARENT[baseKey], px = parentXform[pKey]; if(!px) return;
    // Every uploaded emotion head shares the same face layout. Keep its own expression
    // art, but allow the independent mouth-open patch to animate over neutral/sad/angry/
    // shocked/laughing heads. Custom one-off full-head expressions stay untouched.
    const activeHeadKey = sheetActiveKey('head', char.expr, sh.parts);
    const supportedHead = ['head','head_sad','head_angry','head_shock','head_laugh'].includes(activeHeadKey);
    if(!supportedHead || (char.expr && char.expr.customExpressionId)) return;
    if(!char.expr || char.expr.mouth !== 'open') return;
    const customMouth = sheetRuntimeMouthVariant(sh,char.expr);
    if(customMouth){
      const img=getImg(customMouth.src), headPart=sh.parts[activeHeadKey] || sh.parts.head;
      if(!img.complete || !img.naturalWidth){ if(!img.__hook){img.__hook=true;img.addEventListener('load',()=>{if(typeof drawStick==='function')drawStick();});} return; }
      const preferredKey = char.expr && char.expr.mouth==='open' && sh.parts.mouth_open ? 'mouth_open' : (sh.parts.mouth_closed ? 'mouth_closed' : (sh.parts.mouth_open ? 'mouth_open' : null));
      const baseMouth = preferredKey && sh.parts[preferredKey];
      const nest = preferredKey && sheetNestInfo(sh, preferredKey);
      const mouthDraw=preferredKey && sh.draw && sh.draw[preferredKey];
      const headDraw=sh.draw && (sh.draw[activeHeadKey] || sh.draw.head);
      const mouthPivotX=nest&&Number.isFinite(Number(nest.nestPivotX))?Number(nest.nestPivotX):(mouthDraw&&Number.isFinite(Number(mouthDraw.pvx))?Number(mouthDraw.pvx):Number(baseMouth&&baseMouth.dpx)||0);
      const mouthPivotY=nest&&Number.isFinite(Number(nest.nestPivotY))?Number(nest.nestPivotY):(mouthDraw&&Number.isFinite(Number(mouthDraw.pvy))?Number(mouthDraw.pvy):Number(baseMouth&&baseMouth.dpy)||0);
      const headPivotX=headDraw&&Number.isFinite(Number(headDraw.pvx))?Number(headDraw.pvx):Number(headPart.dpx)||0;
      const headPivotY=headDraw&&Number.isFinite(Number(headDraw.pvy))?Number(headDraw.pvy):Number(headPart.dpy)||0;
      const looksFullHead = Number(customMouth.w||0) >= headPart.w*.82 && Number(customMouth.h||0) >= headPart.h*.82;
      g.save(); g.translate(px.x,px.y); g.rotate(px.rot); g.scale(px.sx,px.sy);
      if(baseMouth && nest && !looksFullHead){
        // Use the exact editor-authored mouth pivot. Using parts[].dpx/dpy here ignored a
        // changed pivot and made the Talk mouth appear beside the nose after saving.
        g.translate(nest.nestDx,nest.nestDy); g.rotate(nest.nestRot); g.scale(nest.nestSx,nest.nestSy);
        g.drawImage(img,-mouthPivotX,-mouthPivotY,baseMouth.w,baseMouth.h);
      }else if(looksFullHead){
        // Backward compatibility for genuinely full-head transparent expression overlays.
        g.drawImage(img,-headPivotX,-headPivotY,headPart.w,headPart.h);
      }else{
        // Legacy 12/14-part models have no mouth crop to inherit. Place a cropped mouth at a
        // conservative lower-face position, retaining the source aspect ratio.
        const mw=Number(customMouth.w)||img.naturalWidth, mh=Number(customMouth.h)||img.naturalHeight;
        const scale=Math.min(headPart.w*.34/Math.max(1,mw),headPart.h*.18/Math.max(1,mh));
        const dw=mw*scale, dh=mh*scale;
        g.drawImage(img,-headPivotX+headPart.w*.5-dw*.5,-headPivotY+headPart.h*.88-dh*.5,dw,dh);
      }
      g.restore(); return;
    }
    const useKey = sheetActiveKey(baseKey, char.expr, sh.parts), part = sh.parts[useKey];
    const nest = part && sheetNestInfo(sh, useKey); if(!part || !nest) return;
    const img = getImg(part.src);
    if(!img.complete || !img.naturalWidth){ if(!img.__hook){ img.__hook = true; img.addEventListener('load', ()=>{ if(typeof drawStick === 'function') drawStick(); }); } return; }
    g.save(); g.translate(px.x, px.y); g.rotate(px.rot); g.scale(px.sx, px.sy);
    g.translate(nest.nestDx,nest.nestDy); g.rotate(nest.nestRot); g.scale(nest.nestSx,nest.nestSy);
    const mouthPivotX=Number.isFinite(Number(nest.nestPivotX))?Number(nest.nestPivotX):Number((sh.draw[useKey]||{}).pvx??part.dpx)||0;
    const mouthPivotY=Number.isFinite(Number(nest.nestPivotY))?Number(nest.nestPivotY):Number((sh.draw[useKey]||{}).pvy??part.dpy)||0;
    g.drawImage(img,-mouthPivotX,-mouthPivotY); g.restore();
  });
  // Every required face-kit asset is still drawn if an older saved layer order omitted it.
  if(isAnimalFaceKit){SHEET_FACE_KIT_NESTED.forEach(drawFaceKitPart);}
  if(isHumanoidFaceKit){['eye_closed_L','eye_closed_R','eye_L','eye_R','brow_L','brow_R'].forEach(drawFaceKitPart);}
  if(isBirdFaceKit){['bird_eye_closed','bird_eye','bird_brow','beak_closed'].forEach(drawFaceKitPart);}
  // Do not paint oval joint patches over source art: they create cross/target marks at wrists and knees.
}

// Joint-bridge helper retained for compatibility with old saved models. The renderer no longer
// invokes these oval patches because they can appear as crosshair/crescent artifacts over imported art.
// Legacy runtime oval-joint blend helpers removed to preserve imported artwork at joins.
// Ambient eye-blinking: independent of whichever animation preset is playing (or none at all), each
// custom character with a head_blink part occasionally swaps to it for a brief moment. Characters
// without that part are untouched forever -- the Math.random() check never even fires for them.
function edScheduleBlinks(){
  const tick = ()=>{
    characters.forEach(c=>{
      // Idle blinks use their own flag (expr.ambientBlink), so they never fight the Talk + Blink clip (expr.blink).
      if(c.type === 'custom' && c.sheet && c.sheet.parts && (c.sheet.parts.head_blink || (c.sheet.parts.eye_L && (c.sheet.parts.eye_closed_L || sheetLatestHeadPart(c.sheet,'blink-head'))) || (c.sheet.parts.eye_L && c.sheet.parts.eye_R && c.sheet.parts.mouth_inner) || c.sheet.parts.bird_eye || sheetLatestHeadPart(c.sheet,'blink-head')) && !(c.expr && c.expr.disableAmbientBlink)){
        if(!c.expr) c.expr = sheetExprFresh();
        if(!c.expr.ambientBlink && Math.random() < 0.55){
          c.expr.ambientBlink = true; drawStick();
          setTimeout(()=>{ c.expr.ambientBlink = false; drawStick(); }, 110+Math.random()*80);
        }
      }
    });
    setTimeout(tick, 2200+Math.random()*2600);
  };
  setTimeout(tick, 1500+Math.random()*1500);
}
function updateModelAssetFromChar(c){
  if(!c || !c.sheet || !c.assetId) return;
  let a = modelAssets.find(x=>String(x.id) === String(c.assetId));
  // A character may originate from an admin-published model. Promote that asset to the local
  // editable library before saving, so a character-side edit is not silently discarded.
  if(!a && Array.isArray(publishedModelAssets)){
    const published = publishedModelAssets.find(x=>String(x.id) === String(c.assetId));
    if(published){ a=JSON.parse(JSON.stringify(published)); delete a.origin; modelAssets.push(a); }
  }
  if(!a) return;
  a.name = c.name || a.name;
  a.updatedAt = Date.now();
  a.sheet = JSON.parse(JSON.stringify(c.sheet));
  a.rigOverride = undefined; a.parts = {};
  a.thumb = c.thumb || c.sheet.thumb;
  a.previewThumb = undefined; // the model was edited; the cached full-body preview is now stale
  a.modelType = c.modelType || (c.sheet && c.sheet.modelType) || a.modelType || 'humanoid';
  saveModelAssetsToStorage();
}

// Initialize only the editor's icons and optional ambient blink loop; do not boot a second lab scene or overwrite characters.
(function initEthvelleSheetEditor(){
  const ready = ()=>{ edFillIcons(); edScheduleBlinks(); };
  if(document.readyState === 'loading') document.addEventListener('DOMContentLoaded', ready, {once:true});
  else ready();
})();
</script>
<script>
/* Ethvelle 21-bone animation refresh, based on the uploaded 14-part Model Lab reference. */
const ETHVELLE_LEGACY_POSE_KEYS_21 = ['spine','armL','armL_lo','armR','armR_lo','legL','legL_lo','legR','legR_lo'];
function ethvelleNormalizeLegacyPoseTo21(p){
  if(!p) return {};
  if(!ETHVELLE_LEGACY_POSE_KEYS_21.some(k=>k in p)) return {...p};
  const o={}, g=k=>Number(p[k])||0, s=g('spine');
  Object.keys(p).forEach(k=>{if(!ETHVELLE_LEGACY_POSE_KEYS_21.includes(k))o[k]=p[k];});
  if('spine' in p){o.spine_lower=(o.spine_lower||0)+s*.4;o.spine_upper=(o.spine_upper||0)+s*.35;o.chest=(o.chest||0)+s*.25;}
  if('head' in p)o.head=(Number(p.head)||0)-s;
  ['L','R'].forEach(S=>{
    const a='arm'+S,al='arm'+S+'_lo',l='leg'+S,ll='leg'+S+'_lo';
    if(a in p||al in p){const u=g(a);o['upperArm'+S]=u-s;o['forearm'+S]=(al in p?g(al):u)-u;}
    if(l in p||ll in p){const u=g(l);o['thigh'+S]=u;o['shin'+S]=(ll in p?g(ll):u)-u;}
  });
  return o;
}
const ethvellePhaseShift21 = p => { const o={}; Object.keys(p).forEach(k=>{ const m=k.match(/^(.*)([LR])$/);
  if(m) o[m[1]+(m[2]==='L'?'R':'L')] = /^clavicle/.test(k) ? -p[k] : p[k]; else o[k] = k==='hipY' ? p[k] : -p[k]; }); return o; };
const ethvelleScalePose21 = (p,f) => { const o={}; Object.keys(p).forEach(k=>o[k]=p[k]*f); return o; };
/* v436: built-in walk / hold-arm pose tables removed - animations now come from the admin panel. */
/* ---------------- Front-view presets + grouping ----------------
   A front-facing character has mirror-image arms and legs. So each front preset is written for the LEFT
   limb only and ethvelleFrontPair21() mirrors it to the right: a right-side angle is the negative of the left one
   (the same motion reflected across the body). Centre bones (pelvis, spine, neck, head) are written as-is. */
const ethvelleFrontPair21 = L => { const o = {}; Object.keys(L).forEach(k => { if(/L$/.test(k)){ o[k] = L[k]; o[k.slice(0,-1)+'R'] = -L[k]; } else o[k] = L[k]; }); return o; };
/* v436: front-view / back-view / updated animation presets removed from this file - they now come from the admin panel. */

/* v436: Beast Men and Lady Walk animations removed from this file - they are now added and controlled in the admin panel (Animations & access). */

function ethvelleIs21Pose(p){
  return !!p && ['pelvis','spine_lower','spine_upper','chest','neck','clavicleL','clavicleR','upperArmL','upperArmR','forearmL','forearmR','thighL','thighR','shinL','shinR','footL','footR'].some(k=>Object.prototype.hasOwnProperty.call(p,k));
}
function ethvelleDiscrete21(preset,tFrac,field){
  const kfs=preset&&preset.keyframes;if(!Array.isArray(kfs))return undefined;
  let val;
  for(let i=0;i<kfs.length;i++)if(kfs[i].t<=tFrac&&kfs[i][field]!==undefined)val=kfs[i][field];
  return val;
}
function ethvelleComplete21Pose(preset,tFrac,pose){
  const p=ethvelleNormalizeLegacyPoseTo21(pose||{});
  if(preset&&preset._ethvelleAnimKey)p.__ethvelleAnimKey=preset._ethvelleAnimKey;
  p.__ethvelleTFrac=((Number(tFrac)||0)%1+1)%1; /* v326: loop position, used for the walk knee bend */
  const fist=ethvelleDiscrete21(preset,tFrac,'fist');
  if(fist!==undefined)p.gestureFist=fist?1:0;
  else p.gestureFist=Number(p.gestureFist)||0;
  p.gesturePoint=Number(p.gesturePoint)||0;
  p.gesturePeace=Number(p.gesturePeace)||0;
  p.gestureThumbsUp=Number(p.gestureThumbsUp)||0;
  const mouth=ethvelleDiscrete21(preset,tFrac,'mouth');
  const blink=ethvelleDiscrete21(preset,tFrac,'blink');
  p.__mouth=mouth===undefined?'closed':mouth;
  p.__blink=blink===undefined?false:!!blink;
  return p;
}

// Updated presets authored for a parent-relative 21-bone rig also need a compatibility pose
// for the original stickman and saved legacy rigs. The sheet renderer uses the full pose below.
function ethvelle21PoseToLegacy(p){
  const out={}, skeleton=new Set(['pelvis','spine_lower','spine_upper','chest','neck','head','clavicleL','clavicleR','upperArmL','upperArmR','forearmL','forearmR','handL','handR','thighL','thighR','shinL','shinR','footL','footR','hipY']);
  Object.keys(p||{}).forEach(k=>{if(!skeleton.has(k)&&!k.startsWith('__ethvelle'))out[k]=p[k];});
  const spine=(Number(p.spine_lower)||0)+(Number(p.spine_upper)||0)+(Number(p.chest)||0);
  out.spine=spine;
  out.head=spine+(Number(p.neck)||0)+(Number(p.head)||0);
  ['L','R'].forEach(S=>{
    const arm=(Number(p['clavicle'+S])||0)+(Number(p['upperArm'+S])||0)+spine;
    out['arm'+S]=arm;
    out['arm'+S+'_lo']=arm+(Number(p['forearm'+S])||0);
    out['hand'+S]=out['arm'+S+'_lo']+(Number(p['hand'+S])||0);
    const leg=(Number(p.pelvis)||0)+(Number(p['thigh'+S])||0);
    out['leg'+S]=leg;
    out['leg'+S+'_lo']=leg+(Number(p['shin'+S])||0)+(Number(p['foot'+S])||0);
  });
  if(p.hipY!==undefined)out.hipY=p.hipY;
  return out;
}

const ethvelleOriginalEvalPreset21 = evalPreset;
evalPreset = function(preset,tFrac){
  const pose=ethvelleOriginalEvalPreset21(preset,tFrac);
  if(preset&&preset._ethvelleRigVersion===21){
    const modern=ethvelleComplete21Pose(preset,tFrac,pose);
    Object.defineProperty(pose,'__ethvelleModernPose',{value:modern,writable:true,configurable:true,enumerable:false});
  }
  return pose;
};

function ethvelleModernPoseForClipAtTime(tSecAbsolute,clipOverride,segmentsOverride){
  const useClip=clipOverride!==undefined?clipOverride:activeClip;
  const useSegments=segmentsOverride!==undefined?segmentsOverride:splitStickmanSegments;
  if(!useClip)return null;
  const hit=stickmanClipAt(tSecAbsolute,useClip,useSegments);if(!hit)return null;
  const clip=hit.clip,preset=animPresets[clip.animKey];
  if(!preset||preset._ethvelleRigVersion!==21)return null;
  const speed=clip.speed||1, localT=Math.max(0,(tSecAbsolute-clip.trimStart)*speed);
  const loopT=(localT%preset.duration)/preset.duration;
  let pose=ethvelleComplete21Pose(preset,loopT,ethvelleOriginalEvalPreset21(preset,loopT));
  if(Array.isArray(clip.keyframes)&&clip.keyframes.length){
    const kfs=[...clip.keyframes].sort((a,b)=>a.t-b.t), WINDOW=.36;
    const holdAndBlend=(kf,base)=>{
      const dist=Math.abs(tSecAbsolute-kf.t);if(dist>=WINDOW)return null;
      const weight=.5*(1+Math.cos(Math.PI*(dist/WINDOW)));
      const kp=ethvelleNormalizeLegacyPoseTo21(kf.pose||{}), blended={};
      const keys=new Set([...Object.keys(base),...Object.keys(kp)]);
      keys.forEach(k=>{
        if(k.startsWith('__'))return;
        const bv=base[k]??0;
        blended[k]=lerp(bv,kp[k]??bv,weight);
      });
      return {...base,...blended};
    };
    if(tSecAbsolute<=kfs[0].t)pose=holdAndBlend(kfs[0],pose)||pose;
    else if(tSecAbsolute>=kfs[kfs.length-1].t)pose=holdAndBlend(kfs[kfs.length-1],pose)||pose;
    else{
      let i=0;while(i<kfs.length-1&&tSecAbsolute>kfs[i+1].t)i++;
      const a=kfs[i],b=kfs[i+1],near=Math.abs(tSecAbsolute-a.t)<=Math.abs(tSecAbsolute-b.t)?a:b,far=near===a?b:a;
      pose=holdAndBlend(near,pose)||holdAndBlend(far,pose)||pose;
    }
    // Preserve preset-level discrete expression fields through the user keyframe blend.
    const mouth=ethvelleDiscrete21(preset,loopT,'mouth'),blink=ethvelleDiscrete21(preset,loopT,'blink'),fist=ethvelleDiscrete21(preset,loopT,'fist');
    pose.__mouth=mouth===undefined?'closed':mouth;pose.__blink=blink===undefined?false:!!blink;
    pose.gestureFist=fist===undefined?(Number(pose.gestureFist)||0):(fist?1:0);
  }
  return pose;
}
const ethvelleOriginalPoseAtClipTime21=poseAtClipTime;
poseAtClipTime=function(tSecAbsolute,clipOverride,segmentsOverride){
  const pose=ethvelleOriginalPoseAtClipTime21(tSecAbsolute,clipOverride,segmentsOverride);
  if(!pose)return pose;
  const modern=ethvelleModernPoseForClipAtTime(tSecAbsolute,clipOverride,segmentsOverride);
  if(modern)Object.defineProperty(pose,'__ethvelleModernPose',{value:modern,writable:true,configurable:true,enumerable:false});
  return pose;
};

const ethvelleOriginalApplyPoseToBones21=applyPoseToBones;
applyPoseToBones=function(pose,targetBones,targetBaseAngles,targetHipRestY){
  const b=targetBones!==undefined?targetBones:bones;
  const character=(Array.isArray(characters)?characters.find(c=>c&&c.bones===b):null)||(b===bones&&typeof activeCharacter==='function'?activeCharacter():null);
  const source=pose||{};
  const modern=source.__ethvelleModernPose||(ethvelleIs21Pose(source)?source:null);
  const legacy=modern?ethvelle21PoseToLegacy(modern):source;
  if(character){
    character._ethvelleModernPose=(character.sheet&&modern)?{...modern}:null;
    if(character.sheet){
      if(!character.expr&&typeof sheetExprFresh==='function')character.expr=sheetExprFresh();
      if(character.expr){
        if(modern){
          character.expr.mouth=modern.__mouth||'closed';
          character.expr.blink=!!modern.__blink;
          const fist=Number(modern.gestureFist)||0,point=Number(modern.gesturePoint)||0,peace=Number(modern.gesturePeace)||0,thumb=Number(modern.gestureThumbsUp)||0;
          character.expr.fistL=character.expr.fistR=fist>.5;
          character.expr.gestL=character.expr.gestR=fist>.5?null:(point>.5?'point':(thumb>.5?'thumb':(peace>.5?'palm':null)));
        }else if(['gestureFist','gesturePoint','gesturePeace','gestureThumbsUp'].some(k=>k in source)){
          const fist=Number(source.gestureFist)||0,point=Number(source.gesturePoint)||0,peace=Number(source.gesturePeace)||0,thumb=Number(source.gestureThumbsUp)||0;
          character.expr.fistL=character.expr.fistR=fist>.5;
          character.expr.gestL=character.expr.gestR=fist>.5?null:(point>.5?'point':(thumb>.5?'thumb':(peace>.5?'palm':null)));
        }
      }
    }
  }
  return ethvelleOriginalApplyPoseToBones21(legacy,targetBones,targetBaseAngles,targetHipRestY);
};

/* v326: knee bend (degrees, backward) for the LEFT-bone leg over one walk loop (0..1).
   The right-bone leg uses the same curve shifted by half a loop. Peak = foot in the air (leg lifting
   and swinging forward), small bend at toe-off, almost straight when the foot is planted. */
const ETHVELLE_WALK_KNEE_KEYS=[[0,12],[0.12,26],[0.25,44],[0.36,46],[0.46,20],[0.5,6],[0.75,3],[1,12]];
function ethvelleWalkKneeFlexDeg(t){
  t=((Number(t)||0)%1+1)%1;
  const k=ETHVELLE_WALK_KNEE_KEYS;
  for(let i=0;i<k.length-1;i++){
    if(t>=k[i][0]&&t<=k[i+1][0]){
      const u=(t-k[i][0])/((k[i+1][0]-k[i][0])||1),e=.5-.5*Math.cos(Math.PI*u);
      return k[i][1]+(k[i+1][1]-k[i][1])*e;
    }
  }
  return k[k.length-1][1];
}
/* v327: hip joint. The two thighs used to pivot too far apart / too low, so a gap opened under the
   pelvis triangle when the legs swung wide. Pull both hip joints a little toward the centre and up so
   the thigh tops stay overlapped with the pelvis piece. The pull grows a bit with the thigh swing.
   Tune with the three numbers below (bigger = closer / higher). */
const ETHVELLE_HIP_IN_REST=0.12, ETHVELLE_HIP_IN_SWING=0.18, ETHVELLE_HIP_UP_REST=0.008, ETHVELLE_HIP_UP_SWING=0.012;
function ethvelleTightenHips(rig,char,p){
  if(!rig||!rig.bones)return;
  const type=(char&&(char.modelType||(char.sheet&&char.sheet.modelType)))||'humanoid';
  if(type!=='humanoid')return;
  const ov=(char&&char.sheet&&char.sheet.rigOverride)||{};
  const scale=stageH*(Number(ov.figFrac)||.5), deg=Math.PI/180;
  ['L','R'].forEach(S=>{
    const b=rig.bones['thigh'+S]; if(!b||!b.off)return;
    const sw=p?Math.min(1,Math.abs(Number(p['thigh'+S])||0)/(24*deg)):0;
    b.off={x:b.off.x*(1-(ETHVELLE_HIP_IN_REST+ETHVELLE_HIP_IN_SWING*sw)),
           y:b.off.y-(ETHVELLE_HIP_UP_REST+ETHVELLE_HIP_UP_SWING*sw)*scale};
  });
}
// Apply the full parent-relative pose directly to the 21-bone runtime used by 14-part sheets.
const ethvelleOriginalSheetRigForCharacter21=sheetRigForCharacter;
sheetRigForCharacter=function(char){
  const rig=ethvelleOriginalSheetRigForCharacter21(char);
  const p=char&&char._ethvelleModernPose;
  if(!p||!rig||!rig.bones||!rig.baseAngles){ethvelleTightenHips(rig,char,null);return rig;}
  ethvelleTightenHips(rig,char,p);
  const clamp=(v,lo,hi)=>Math.max(lo,Math.min(hi,v));
  const deg=Math.PI/180;
  const limits={pelvis:28,spine_lower:28,spine_upper:28,chest:30,neck:35,head:42,clavicleL:28,clavicleR:28,upperArmL:165,upperArmR:165,forearmL:145,forearmR:145,handL:95,handR:95,thighL:125,thighR:125,shinL:145,shinR:145,footL:85,footR:85};
  // v326 WALK KNEES: while a leg is lifted (swing phase) the knee must fold BACKWARD (shin goes behind
  // the thigh), never forward. The old preset values made one leg bend forward and used a one-leg
  // sign flip. For the walk presets (all views except back) the shin angle is now driven by a
  // swing-phase curve: both knees only ever fold backward, the most when the foot is in the air,
  // and are almost straight when the foot is planted. Thigh, foot placement, arms and every other
  // animation stay exactly as they were.
  const walkKneeFix=String((char&&char.sheet&&char.sheet.view)||'front')!=='back' &&
    (char.modelType||(char.sheet&&char.sheet.modelType)||'humanoid')==='humanoid' &&
    ['walk','walkHold','frontWalk','ladyWalk','beastLadyWalk'].includes(p.__ethvelleAnimKey) && typeof p.__ethvelleTFrac==='number';
  // v369: Lady walk reuses the same backward-only knee curve, but only ~45% as deep, so the knees barely lift
  // and the saree / long-dress hem is never dragged far out of shape.
  const kneeScale=p.__ethvelleAnimKey==='frontWalk'?0.8:(p.__ethvelleAnimKey==='ladyWalk'||p.__ethvelleAnimKey==='beastLadyWalk')?0.45:1;
  const kneeFlex={
    shinL:walkKneeFix?ethvelleWalkKneeFlexDeg(p.__ethvelleTFrac)*kneeScale*deg:0,
    shinR:walkKneeFix?ethvelleWalkKneeFlexDeg(p.__ethvelleTFrac+0.5)*kneeScale*deg:0
  };
  Object.keys(rig.bones).forEach(k=>{
    if(k==='root'||rig.baseAngles[k]===undefined)return;
    const raw=Number(p[k])||0;
    let poseDelta=raw;
    if(walkKneeFix){
      if(k==='shinL'||k==='shinR')poseDelta=kneeFlex[k];                       // knee folds back only
      else if(k==='footL'||k==='footR')poseDelta=raw*0.4-0.45*kneeFlex['shin'+k.slice(-1)]; // keep the shoe from flipping up
      else if(k==='forearmL'||k==='forearmR')poseDelta=-(5*deg+0.9*Math.abs(raw));      // v327: elbow folds FORWARD only, never back
    }
    const limit=(limits[k]||165)*deg,delta=clamp(poseDelta,-limit,limit);
    rig.bones[k].angle=rig.baseAngles[k]+delta;
  });
  return rig;
};

const ethvelleOriginalResetTimelineFrame21=resetCharacterForTimelineFrame;
resetCharacterForTimelineFrame=function(char){if(char)char._ethvelleModernPose=null;return ethvelleOriginalResetTimelineFrame21(char);};
const ethvelleOriginalResetBonesToRest21=resetCharacterBonesToRest;
resetCharacterBonesToRest=function(char){if(char)char._ethvelleModernPose=null;return ethvelleOriginalResetBonesToRest21(char);};
const ethvelleOriginalCancelPreview21=cancelAnimationPreview;
cancelAnimationPreview=function(redraw){
  const oldId=previewCharacterId;
  ethvelleOriginalCancelPreview21(redraw);
  const c=oldId&&Array.isArray(characters)?characters.find(ch=>ch&&ch.id===oldId):activeCharacter();
  if(c&&c.sheet){
    c._ethvelleModernPose=null;
    const isAnimalFaceKit=String(c.modelType||(c.sheet&&c.sheet.modelType)||'')==='animal'&&c.sheet.parts&&c.sheet.parts.eye_L&&c.sheet.parts.eye_R;
    // cancelAnimationPreview has already restored the saved expression snapshot for this kit.
    // Do not replace that snapshot with legacy closed-mouth / non-blinking defaults.
    if(c.expr&&!isAnimalFaceKit){c.expr.mouth='closed';c.expr.blink=false;c.expr.fistL=false;c.expr.fistR=false;c.expr.gestL=null;c.expr.gestR=null;}
  }
  if(redraw!==false&&c&&c.sheet&&typeof drawStick==='function')drawStick();
};

// The sheet renderer's expression data is authoritative for imported cut-out faces; this keeps
// the legacy face overlay's mouth animation in sync for older custom rigs too.
try{
  drawEthvelleFaceMotion = (function(original){
    return function(ctx,char,start,end){return original(ctx,char,start,end);};
  })(drawEthvelleFaceMotion);
}catch(_e){}
</script>

<script id="ethvelle-text-shadow-owner">
/* Paint text shadow on the complete text composite, not on the fill glyph.  The composite contains
   the independently-rendered outer-stroke clone followed by the fill glyph, so filter:drop-shadow()
   paints the shadow behind both.  This is also used by the composer mirror preview. */
(function(){
  'use strict';
  window.ethvelleApplyTextShadow=function(el,shadow,hasFill,important){
    if(!el||!el.style)return;
    var css=(shadow==null||shadow===''||shadow==='none')?'none':String(shadow);
    var pr=important?'important':'';
    var host=el;
    if(el.classList&&el.classList.contains('evpa-text-effect-layer')){
      var p=el.parentElement;
      if(p&&p.classList&&p.classList.contains('ethvelle-overlay-text-content'))host=p;
    }
    if(host!==el){
      el.style.setProperty('text-shadow','none',pr);
      el.style.setProperty('filter','none',pr);
      if(el.dataset)delete el.dataset.evShadowFilter;
    }
    if(css!=='none'){
      host.style.setProperty('text-shadow','none',pr);
      host.style.setProperty('filter','drop-shadow('+css+')',pr);
      if(host.dataset)host.dataset.evShadowFilter='1';
    }else{
      host.style.setProperty('text-shadow','none',pr);
      if(host.dataset&&host.dataset.evShadowFilter==='1'){
        host.style.removeProperty('filter');delete host.dataset.evShadowFilter;
      }
    }
  };
})();
</script>
<script id="ethvelle-text-editor-indigo-v6">
(function(){
  const EV_COLORS=['#FFFFFF','#000000','#A3A3A3','#414141','#FFC5CF','#FA847C','#FF4E57','#FF174D','#D70D20','#FF7B24','#FFD43B','#B5E53B','#25C98A','#16D9D0','#13A7FF','#2F5CE8','#605DFF','#875BFA','#D946EF'];
  const EV_FONTS=[
    ['Inter','Inter  -  Default'],['Poppins','Poppins'],['Montserrat','Montserrat'],['Roboto','Roboto'],['Lato','Lato'],['Oswald','Oswald'],['Sora','Sora'],
    ['Hind Siliguri','Hind Siliguri'],['Noto Sans Bengali','Noto Sans Bengali'],['Noto Serif Bengali','Noto Serif Bengali'],
    ['Anek Bangla','Anek Bangla'],['Alkatra','Alkatra'],['Atma','Atma'],['Baloo Da 2','Baloo Da 2'],['Galada','Galada'],['Google Sans','Google Sans'],['Mina','Mina'],['Tiro Bangla','Tiro Bangla'],
    ['Solaiman Lipi','Solaiman Lipi'],
    ['Kalpurush','Kalpurush'],
    ['Siyam Rupali','Siyam Rupali'],
    ['Lohit Bengali','Lohit Bengali'],
    ['Bornomala','Bornomala'],
    ['Bornomala Vintage','Bornomala Vintage'],
    ['Ekushey Lal Sabuj','Ekushey Lal Sabuj'],
    ['Adorsho Lipi','Adorsho Lipi'],
    ['Charukola','Charukola'],
    ['Ekushey Mukto','Ekushey Mukto'],
    ['Bensen','Bensen'],
    ['Bensen Handwriting','Bensen Handwriting'],
    ['Ekushey Saraswatii','Ekushey Saraswatii'],
    ['Ekushey Azad','Ekushey Azad'],
    ['Hoogli','Hoogli'],
    ['Bornoporichay','Bornoporichay'],
    ['Aikya','Aikya'],
    ['Kalaa','Kalaa'],
    ['UN Bangla','UN Bangla'],
    ['Tuli','Tuli']
  ];
  let draft=null, root=null, styleRoot=null, pickerRoot=null, activeColorKey='textColor', pickHue=0, pickSat=100, pickLight=55, pickOpacity=100, pickHex='#FF4E57', customColors=[];
  let oldOpenPanel=window.openPanel, oldSyncBottomToolsMode=window.syncBottomToolsMode;
  customColors=[];
  function getSelected(){try{return typeof selectedTextOverlay==='function'?selectedTextOverlay():null}catch(_){return null}}
  function currentTarget(){return root&&root.classList.contains('active')&&draft?draft:getSelected()}
  function safeHex(hex){return /^#[0-9a-f]{6}$/i.test(String(hex||''))?String(hex):'#FFFFFF'}
  function alphaKeyFor(key){return key==='textColor'?'textOpacity':key==='strokeColor'?'strokeOpacity':key==='shadowColor'?'shadowOpacity':key==='textBackgroundColor'?'textBackgroundOpacity':null;}
  function targetColor(){const t=currentTarget();return t?safeHex(t[activeColorKey]||'#FFFFFF'):'#FFFFFF';}
  function hslToHex(h,s,l){s/=100;l/=100;const k=n=>(n+h/30)%12,a=s*Math.min(l,1-l),f=n=>{const c=k(n);return l-a*Math.max(-1,Math.min(c-3,Math.min(9-c,1)));};return '#'+[f(0),f(8),f(4)].map(v=>Math.round(v*255).toString(16).padStart(2,'0')).join('').toUpperCase();}
  function hexToHsl(hex){const h=safeHex(hex).slice(1),r=parseInt(h.slice(0,2),16)/255,g=parseInt(h.slice(2,4),16)/255,b=parseInt(h.slice(4,6),16)/255,max=Math.max(r,g,b),min=Math.min(r,g,b),d=max-min;let hue=0,sat=0,light=(max+min)/2;if(d){sat=d/(1-Math.abs(2*light-1));switch(max){case r:hue=((g-b)/d)%6;break;case g:hue=(b-r)/d+2;break;default:hue=(r-g)/d+4;}hue*=60;if(hue<0)hue+=360;}return {h:hue,s:sat*100,l:light*100};}
  function updateColorTarget(key,hex,opacity){const t=currentTarget();if(!t)return;t[key]=safeHex(hex);const akey=alphaKeyFor(key);if(akey&&opacity!=null)t[akey]=Math.max(0,Math.min(100,Number(opacity)));if(root&&root.classList.contains('active')){fitComposerText();if(key==='textColor'){const slider=document.getElementById('evtcTextOpacity'),label=document.getElementById('evtcTextOpacityValue'),clear=document.querySelector('.evtc-transparent-swatch');if(slider)slider.value=t.textOpacity;if(label)label.textContent=Math.round(t.textOpacity)+'%';if(clear)clear.classList.toggle('active',Number(t.textOpacity)===0);}}else{if(typeof renderFrame==='function')renderFrame();if(typeof persistProjectState==='function')persistProjectState({skipHistory:true});}}
  function rememberProjectSampleColor(value){const c=safeHex(value);try{customColors=window.__ethvelleGetProjectCustomColors?window.__ethvelleGetProjectCustomColors():customColors;}catch(_e){}if(!customColors.some(v=>String(v).toUpperCase()===c.toUpperCase()))customColors.push(c);customColors=customColors.slice(-12);try{localStorage.setItem('ethvelleCustomTextColors',JSON.stringify(customColors));}catch(_e){}renderColorStrip();}
  function renderColorStrip(){const host=document.getElementById('evtcColorStrip');if(!host)return;try{customColors=window.__ethvelleGetProjectCustomColors?window.__ethvelleGetProjectCustomColors():customColors;}catch(_e){}const colors=[...new Set([...EV_COLORS,...customColors])];host.innerHTML=`<button type="button" class="evtc-swatch-action evtc-wheel-swatch" aria-label="Custom color wheel" title="Custom color" onclick="openEthvelleColorPicker('textColor')"></button><button type="button" class="evtc-swatch-action evtc-transparent-swatch" aria-label="Transparent text color" title="Transparent" onclick="setEthvelleComposerOpacity(0)"></button>`+colors.map(c=>`<button type="button" class="evtc-color-swatch" data-color="${c}" style="background:${c}" aria-label="Text colour ${c}" onclick="setEthvelleComposerColor('${c}')"></button>`).join('');}
  window.ethvelleHexToRgba=function(hex,alpha){
    const h=safeHex(hex).slice(1);const r=parseInt(h.slice(0,2),16),g=parseInt(h.slice(2,4),16),b=parseInt(h.slice(4,6),16);
    return `rgba(${r}, ${g}, ${b}, ${Math.max(0,Math.min(1,Number(alpha)||0))})`;
  };
  function copyStyle(ov){
    const o=ov||{};
    return {editingId:ov?ov.id:null,textContent:o.textContent||'',fontFamily:o.fontFamily||(typeof pendingAdminFontFamily!=='undefined'&&pendingAdminFontFamily)||'Inter',fontSize:Math.max(12,Math.min(120,Number(o.fontSize)||44)),fontWeight:Number(o.fontWeight)||800,fontStyle:o.fontStyle||'normal',textAlign:o.textAlign||'center',textColor:o.textColor||'#FFFFFF',textOpacity:Number(o.textOpacity==null?100:o.textOpacity),strokeWidth:Math.max(0,Number(o.strokeWidth)||0),strokeColor:o.strokeColor||'#16162A',strokeOpacity:Number(o.strokeOpacity==null?100:o.strokeOpacity),shadowEnabled:!!o.shadowEnabled,shadowColor:o.shadowColor||'#000000',shadowOpacity:Number(o.shadowOpacity==null?100:o.shadowOpacity),shadowBlur:Math.max(0,Number(o.shadowBlur)||6),shadowX:Number(o.shadowX)||0,shadowY:Math.max(4,Number(o.shadowY)||6),textBackgroundEnabled:!!o.textBackgroundEnabled,textBackgroundColor:o.textBackgroundColor||'#182A66',textBackgroundOpacity:Number(o.textBackgroundOpacity==null?78:o.textBackgroundOpacity),textBackgroundPadding:Number(o.textBackgroundPadding==null?8:o.textBackgroundPadding),textBackgroundRadius:Number(o.textBackgroundRadius==null?8:o.textBackgroundRadius),textBackgroundShape:o.textBackgroundShape||'rounded',transform:o.transform?Object.assign({},o.transform):{scale:1,offsetX:0,offsetY:0,rotation:0}};
  }
  function ensureRoot(){
    if(root)return root;
    root=document.createElement('div');root.id='ethvelleTextComposer';root.className='evtc-root';root.setAttribute('aria-hidden','true');
    root.innerHTML=`
      <div class="evtc-scrim"></div>
      <header class="evtc-header">
        <button type="button" class="evtc-head-btn" aria-label="Cancel text editing" onclick="closeEthvelleTextComposer(false)"><svg viewBox="0 0 24 24"><path d="M18 6L6 18M6 6l12 12"/></svg></button>
        <div class="evtc-head-center" aria-hidden="true"><svg viewBox="0 0 24 24"><path d="M4 7h16M2 12h20M5 17h14"/><path d="M8 4h8M8 20h8"/></svg></div>
        <button type="button" class="evtc-head-btn" aria-label="Add text" onclick="saveEthvelleTextComposer()"><svg viewBox="0 0 24 24"><path d="M4 12.5l5.2 5.2L20 6.8"/></svg></button>
      </header>
      <div id="evtcStageBackplate" class="evtc-stage-backplate" aria-hidden="true"></div>
      <textarea id="evtcStageText" class="evtc-stage-text" rows="2" maxlength="240" placeholder="Enter your text" spellcheck="false" aria-label="Enter your text" oninput="updateEthvelleComposerText()"></textarea>
      <div class="evtc-hint">Type your text, then tap ✓ to add it to the timeline</div>
      <div class="evtc-dock" id="evtcDock">
        <div class="evtc-dock-tools">
          <button type="button" class="evtc-tool-btn" onclick="openEthvelleTextStylePanel('font')"><b>Aa</b><span>Font</span></button>
          <button type="button" class="evtc-tool-btn" onclick="ethvelleComposerToggleBold()"><b>B</b><span>Bold</span></button>
          <button type="button" class="evtc-tool-btn" onclick="openEthvelleTextStylePanel('stroke')"><svg viewBox="0 0 24 24"><circle cx="12" cy="12" r="8"/><circle cx="12" cy="12" r="4"/></svg><span>Stroke</span></button>
          <button type="button" class="evtc-tool-btn" onclick="openEthvelleTextStylePanel('shadow')"><svg viewBox="0 0 24 24"><path d="M5 5h10v10H5z"/><path d="M9 9h10v10H9z"/></svg><span>Shadow</span></button>
          <button type="button" class="evtc-tool-btn" onclick="openEthvelleTextStylePanel('background')"><svg viewBox="0 0 24 24"><rect x="3" y="4" width="18" height="16" rx="3"/><path d="M6 15h12"/></svg><span>BG</span></button>
        </div>
        <div class="evtc-color-strip" id="evtcColorStrip"></div>
        <div class="evtc-opacity-line"><span>Opacity</span><input id="evtcTextOpacity" type="range" min="0" max="100" value="100" oninput="setEthvelleComposerOpacity(this.value)"><output id="evtcTextOpacityValue">100%</output></div>
      </div>`;
    document.body.appendChild(root);
    renderColorStrip();
    root.querySelector('.evtc-scrim').addEventListener('click',()=>window.closeEthvelleTextComposer(false));
    return root;
  }
  function syncLayout(){
    if(!root||!root.classList.contains('active'))return;
    const input=document.getElementById('evtcStageText');const stage=document.getElementById('canvasStage');
    const vv=window.visualViewport;
    // Once the user starts typing, Android may shrink the visual viewport for the keyboard.
    // Keep the full-screen composer and the writing field at their original geometry instead
    // of repeatedly recalculating the canvas/text size on every keyboard resize/input event.
    const activelyTyping=!!input&&document.activeElement===input;
    // Track the visible viewport even while typing: Android shrinks visualViewport when
    // the soft keyboard opens, so the bottom text palette remains floating above it.
    if(vv){root.style.top=vv.offsetTop+'px';root.style.height=vv.height+'px';root.style.bottom='auto';}
    else {root.style.top='0px';root.style.height='100dvh';root.style.bottom='auto';}
    if(stage&&input&&!activelyTyping){
      const r=stage.getBoundingClientRect();
      // Normally this is the live canvas stage. If the editor is still completing its first
      // layout (or mobile keyboard resize briefly reports a zero-size stage), keep the input
      // centered and fully usable instead of collapsing it to a tiny single-character field.
      const wrap=document.querySelector('.canvas-wrap');const wr=wrap?wrap.getBoundingClientRect():{width:0,height:0};
      const fallbackW=Math.min(Math.max(240,wr.width||0),Math.max(240,window.innerWidth*.92));
      const w=r.width>80?r.width:fallbackW;
      const h=r.height>80?r.height:Math.min(window.innerHeight*.44,w*9/16);
      const left=r.width>80?r.left:(window.innerWidth-w)/2;
      const top=r.height>80?r.top:Math.max(82,Math.min(window.innerHeight*.34,window.innerHeight*.28));
      input.style.left=Math.max(0,left+w*.045)+'px';
      input.style.top=(top+h*.20)+'px';
      input.style.width=Math.max(150,w*.91)+'px';
      input.style.height=Math.max(76,h*.60)+'px';
      input.style.fontSize=Math.max(22,Math.min(56,Number(draft&&draft.fontSize)||44,w*.095))+'px';
    }
  }
  function installLayoutListeners(){
    window.addEventListener('resize',syncLayout,{passive:true});
    if(window.visualViewport)window.visualViewport.addEventListener('resize',syncLayout,{passive:true});
    if(window.visualViewport)window.visualViewport.addEventListener('scroll',syncLayout,{passive:true});
  }
  installLayoutListeners();
  window.openEthvelleTextComposer=function(id){
    ensureRoot();
    const ov=(id?overlays.find(o=>o.id===id&&o.type==='text'):getSelected())||null;
    draft=copyStyle(ov);
    if(!draft.fontFamily)draft.fontFamily='Inter';
    root.classList.add('active');root.setAttribute('aria-hidden','false');
    const ed=document.getElementById('screen-editor');if(ed)ed.classList.add('ev-text-backdrop-blur');
    document.body.classList.add('evtc-is-open');
    const input=document.getElementById('evtcStageText');
    input.value=draft.textContent||'';input.style.color=window.ethvelleHexToRgba(draft.textColor,Number(draft.textOpacity==null?100:draft.textOpacity)/100);input.style.fontFamily='"'+draft.fontFamily+'",sans-serif';input.style.fontWeight=String(draft.fontWeight||800);input.style.fontStyle=draft.fontStyle||'normal';input.style.textAlign=draft.textAlign||'center';
    input.style.webkitTextStroke=draft.strokeWidth>0?`${draft.strokeWidth}px ${window.ethvelleHexToRgba(draft.strokeColor,Number(draft.strokeOpacity==null?100:draft.strokeOpacity)/100)}`:'0 transparent';
    input.style.textShadow=draft.shadowEnabled?`${Number(draft.shadowX)||0}px ${Math.max(4,Number(draft.shadowY)||6)}px ${Math.max(0,Number(draft.shadowBlur)||0)}px ${window.ethvelleHexToRgba(draft.shadowColor,Number(draft.shadowOpacity==null?100:draft.shadowOpacity)/100)}`:'none';
    input.style.backgroundColor='transparent';
    input.style.padding=draft.textBackgroundEnabled?draft.textBackgroundPadding+'px':'0';input.style.borderRadius=draft.textBackgroundRadius+'px';
    const op=document.getElementById('evtcTextOpacity'),opv=document.getElementById('evtcTextOpacityValue');if(op)op.value=Number(draft.textOpacity==null?100:draft.textOpacity);if(opv)opv.textContent=Math.round(Number(draft.textOpacity==null?100:draft.textOpacity))+'%';
    document.querySelectorAll('#evtcColorStrip .evtc-color-swatch').forEach(b=>b.classList.toggle('active',String(b.dataset.color).toLowerCase()===String(draft.textColor||'#FFFFFF').toLowerCase()));
    syncLayout();syncComposerBackplate();
    setTimeout(()=>{syncLayout();syncComposerBackplate();/* Do not auto-focus here: opening the panel should not summon the mobile keyboard or shift the canvas. The user can tap the text field when ready to type. */},80);
  };
  window.closeEthvelleTextComposer=function(save){
    if(root){root.classList.remove('active');root.setAttribute('aria-hidden','true');}
    const ed=document.getElementById('screen-editor');if(ed)ed.classList.remove('ev-text-backdrop-blur');
    document.body.classList.remove('evtc-is-open');
    if(styleRoot&&styleRoot.classList.contains('evts-compose-subpanel'))closeEthvelleTextStylePanel();
    draft=null;
    if(typeof syncBottomToolsMode==='function')syncBottomToolsMode();
  };
  window.updateEthvelleComposerText=function(){if(!draft)return;const i=document.getElementById('evtcStageText');draft.textContent=i?i.value:'';fitComposerText();};
  function syncComposerBackplate(){
    const input=document.getElementById('evtcStageText'),plate=document.getElementById('evtcStageBackplate'),t=draft;
    if(!input||!plate||!t){if(plate)plate.style.display='none';return;}
    const active=!!t.textBackgroundEnabled;
    if(!active){plate.style.display='none';return;}
    const r=input.getBoundingClientRect();
    plate.style.display='block';plate.style.left=r.left+'px';plate.style.top=r.top+'px';plate.style.width=r.width+'px';plate.style.height=r.height+'px';
    plate.style.backgroundColor=window.ethvelleHexToRgba(t.textBackgroundColor||'#182A66',1);
    plate.style.borderRadius=(Number(t.textBackgroundRadius)||8)+'px';
    const shape=String(t.textBackgroundShape||'rounded');
    const mask=window.ethvelleTextBgMaskCss?window.ethvelleTextBgMaskCss(shape):'none';
    plate.style.webkitMaskImage=mask;plate.style.maskImage=mask;plate.style.webkitMaskSize='100% 100%';plate.style.maskSize='100% 100%';plate.style.webkitMaskRepeat='no-repeat';plate.style.maskRepeat='no-repeat';
    plate.style.opacity=String(Math.max(0,Math.min(100,Number(t.textBackgroundOpacity==null?78:t.textBackgroundOpacity)))/100);
  }
  function fitComposerText(){
    if(!draft)return;const i=document.getElementById('evtcStageText');if(!i)return;
    const stage=document.getElementById('canvasStage');const r=stage?stage.getBoundingClientRect():{width:window.innerWidth};
    i.style.fontSize=Math.max(22,Math.min(56,Number(draft.fontSize)||44))+'px';i.style.color=window.ethvelleHexToRgba(draft.textColor||'#FFFFFF',Number(draft.textOpacity==null?100:draft.textOpacity)/100);i.style.fontFamily='"'+(draft.fontFamily||'Inter')+'",sans-serif';i.style.fontWeight=String(draft.fontWeight||800);i.style.fontStyle=draft.fontStyle||'normal';i.style.textAlign=draft.textAlign||'center';
    i.style.webkitTextStroke=Number(draft.strokeWidth)>0?`${draft.strokeWidth}px ${window.ethvelleHexToRgba(draft.strokeColor||'#16162A',Number(draft.strokeOpacity==null?100:draft.strokeOpacity)/100)}`:'0 transparent';
    window.ethvelleApplyTextShadow(i,draft.shadowEnabled?`${Number(draft.shadowX)||0}px ${Math.max(4,Number(draft.shadowY)||6)}px ${Math.max(0,Number(draft.shadowBlur)||0)}px ${window.ethvelleHexToRgba(draft.shadowColor||'#000000',Number(draft.shadowOpacity==null?100:draft.shadowOpacity)/100)}`:'none',i.dataset.evFill==='1');
    i.style.backgroundColor='transparent';
    i.style.padding=draft.textBackgroundEnabled?`${Number(draft.textBackgroundPadding)||0}px`:'0';i.style.borderRadius=(Number(draft.textBackgroundRadius)||0)+'px';
    syncLayout();syncComposerBackplate();
  }
  window.setEthvelleComposerColor=function(c){if(!draft)return;draft.textColor=safeHex(c);if(Number(draft.textOpacity)===0)draft.textOpacity=100;activeColorKey='textColor';document.querySelectorAll('#evtcColorStrip .evtc-color-swatch').forEach(b=>b.classList.toggle('active',String(b.dataset.color||'').toLowerCase()===draft.textColor.toLowerCase()));const clear=document.getElementById('evtcTextOpacity');const out=document.getElementById('evtcTextOpacityValue');if(clear)clear.value=draft.textOpacity;if(out)out.textContent=Math.round(draft.textOpacity)+'%';fitComposerText();};
  window.setEthvelleComposerOpacity=function(value){if(!draft)return;draft.textOpacity=Math.max(0,Math.min(100,Number(value)));const out=document.getElementById('evtcTextOpacityValue');if(out)out.textContent=Math.round(draft.textOpacity)+'%';const slider=document.getElementById('evtcTextOpacity');if(slider)slider.value=draft.textOpacity;const clear=document.querySelector('.evtc-transparent-swatch');if(clear)clear.classList.toggle('active',draft.textOpacity===0);fitComposerText();};
  window.addEthvelleCustomPaletteColor=function(){const t=currentTarget();const c=safeHex(t?(t[activeColorKey]||t.textColor):'#FFFFFF');try{customColors=window.__ethvelleGetProjectCustomColors?window.__ethvelleGetProjectCustomColors():customColors;}catch(_e){}if(!customColors.some(x=>x.toLowerCase()===c.toLowerCase()))customColors.push(c);customColors=customColors.slice(-12);try{localStorage.setItem('ethvelleCustomTextColors',JSON.stringify(customColors));}catch(_e){}renderColorStrip();const active=document.querySelector(`#evtcColorStrip [data-color="${c}"]`);if(active)active.classList.add('active');if(typeof showToast==='function')showToast('Color saved to palette',{icon:toastIcons.check,duration:1200});};
  window.ethvelleComposerToggleBold=function(){if(!draft)return;draft.fontWeight=Number(draft.fontWeight)>=700?500:800;fitComposerText();};
  window.saveEthvelleTextComposer=function(){
    if(!draft)return;
    const wasEditing=!!draft.editingId;
    const text=String(draft.textContent||'').trim();
    if(!text){if(typeof showToast==='function')showToast('Write some text first',{icon:toastIcons.info,duration:1500});return;}
    let clip=draft.editingId?overlays.find(o=>o.id===draft.editingId&&o.type==='text'):null;
    if(!clip){
      const len=5,startT=(typeof window.ethvellePlanTextInsertStart==='function'?window.ethvellePlanTextInsertStart(len):Math.max(0,Number(currentAbsoluteTimeSec)||0));
      clip={id:'ov'+Date.now()+Math.random().toString(36).slice(2,5),name:text.replace(/\s+/g,' ').slice(0,22)||'Text',color:'#454B73',type:'text',sourceDuration:len,trimStart:startT,trimEnd:startT+len,thumbs:[],peaks:[],speed:1,transform:{scale:1,offsetX:0,offsetY:0,rotation:0}};
      overlays.push(clip);
    }
    ['textContent','fontFamily','fontSize','fontWeight','fontStyle','textAlign','textColor','textOpacity','strokeWidth','strokeColor','strokeOpacity','shadowEnabled','shadowColor','shadowOpacity','shadowBlur','shadowX','shadowY','textBackgroundEnabled','textBackgroundColor','textBackgroundOpacity','textBackgroundPadding','textBackgroundRadius','textBackgroundShape'].forEach(k=>{if(draft[k]!==undefined)clip[k]=draft[k];});
    clip.textContent=text;clip.name=text.replace(/\s+/g,' ').slice(0,22)||'Text';clip.color='#605DFF';
    ensureAdminFontLoaded(clip.fontFamily||'Inter');
    timelineDuration=Math.max(timelineDuration,clip.trimEnd);
    if(typeof renderOverlayLayers==='function')renderOverlayLayers();if(typeof syncTimelineInnerWidth==='function')syncTimelineInnerWidth();if(typeof renderRuler==='function')renderRuler();
    if(typeof onClipSelected==='function')onClipSelected(clip,wasEditing?'select':'add');else if(typeof selectClipForChrome==='function')selectClipForChrome(clip.id);
    if(typeof renderFrame==='function')renderFrame();if(typeof persistProjectState==='function')persistProjectState();
    window.closeEthvelleTextComposer(true);
    if(typeof showToast==='function')showToast(wasEditing?'Text updated':'Text added',{icon:toastIcons.check,duration:1200});
  };
  function addTextTools(){
    const row=document.getElementById('bottomToolsClip');if(!row||document.getElementById('clipTextEditTool'))return;
    const make=(id,label,icon,tab)=>{const b=document.createElement('div');b.className='tool-item ev-text-tool';b.id=id;b.setAttribute('role','button');b.tabIndex=0;b.innerHTML=icon+`<span>${label}</span>`;b.onclick=()=>tab==='edit'?window.openEthvelleTextComposer(selectedClip):window.openEthvelleTextStylePanel(tab);b.onkeydown=e=>{if(e.key==='Enter'||e.key===' '){e.preventDefault();b.click();}};b.style.display='none';return b;};
    const items=[
      make('clipTextEditTool','Edit text','<svg viewBox="0 0 24 24" fill="none" stroke-width="1.8" stroke-linecap="round" stroke-linejoin="round"><path d="M4 7V4h16v3M9 20h6M12 4v16"/></svg>','edit'),
      make('clipTextFontTool','Font','<svg viewBox="0 0 24 24" fill="none" stroke-width="1.8" stroke-linecap="round" stroke-linejoin="round"><path d="M4 20L10 4h4l6 16M7 14h10"/></svg>','font'),
      make('clipTextStrokeTool','Stroke','<svg viewBox="0 0 24 24" fill="none" stroke-width="1.8"><circle cx="12" cy="12" r="8"/><circle cx="12" cy="12" r="4"/></svg>','stroke'),
      make('clipTextShadowTool','Shadow','<svg viewBox="0 0 24 24" fill="none" stroke-width="1.8" stroke-linejoin="round"><rect x="4" y="4" width="11" height="11" rx="1.5"/><rect x="9" y="9" width="11" height="11" rx="1.5"/></svg>','shadow'),
      make('clipTextColorTool','Color','<svg viewBox="0 0 24 24" fill="none" stroke-width="1.8" stroke-linecap="round" stroke-linejoin="round"><path d="M12 3a9 9 0 1 0 0 18h1a2 2 0 0 0 1-3.7 1.7 1.7 0 0 1 1.3-3.3H17a4 4 0 0 0 4-4c0-4-4-7-9-7z"/><circle cx="7.5" cy="10" r="1"/><circle cx="11" cy="6.8" r="1"/><circle cx="16" cy="8" r="1"/></svg>','color'),
      make('clipTextBackgroundTool','Text BG','<svg viewBox="0 0 24 24" fill="none" stroke-width="1.8" stroke-linejoin="round"><rect x="3" y="4" width="18" height="16" rx="3"/><path d="M6 15h12"/></svg>','background')
    ];
    items.reverse().forEach(b=>row.insertBefore(b,row.firstChild));
  }
  function setTextTools(){
    addTextTools();const clip=getSelected();const ids=['clipTextEditTool','clipTextFontTool','clipTextStrokeTool','clipTextShadowTool','clipTextColorTool','clipTextBackgroundTool'];
    const visible=!!clip&&!!selectedClip&&String(clip.id)===String(selectedClip)&&!root?.classList.contains('active');
    ids.forEach(id=>{const el=document.getElementById(id);if(el)el.style.display=visible?'flex':'none';});
    const hideForText=['clipAnimTool','clipTrimTool','clipSpeedTool','clipVolumeTool','clipChromaTool','clipExtractAudioTool','clipAddMediaTool','clipSplitTool'];
    hideForText.forEach(id=>{const el=document.getElementById(id);if(el){if(visible)el.style.display='none';else if(typeof oldSyncBottomToolsMode==='function'&&selectedClip){/* the original sync function repopulates these on every state transition */}}});
    if(!visible&&typeof oldSyncBottomToolsMode==='function'&&selectedClip){
      // Re-run original visibility rules for non-text selections, without recursively invoking this wrapper.
      try{oldSyncBottomToolsMode.call(window)}catch(_e){}
    }
